# CLAUDE.md

Guidance for Claude Code in this repository. This file is loaded into every session, so it is kept to **what is true across the whole system**. Per-feature history, file lists and test counts live elsewhere:

| Need | Read |
|---|---|
| What exists in code, file by file, per feature | `docs/architecture/system-overview.md` |
| Why a technical decision was made | `docs/adr/INDEX.md` → `docs/adr/000n-*.md` |
| Known code debt (placeholders, deferred work, assumptions) | `docs/dept/INDEX.md` → `D<nnn>-*.md` (gitignored, local only) |
| Task plan / status / deadlines | `docs/time.md`, `docs/timeline_petcare_v15.xlsx` |
| Who calls which cross-module interface, delivery dates | `docs/06-module-contracts.md` |

## Project

Pet Care — management system for a chain of pet clinics: exam / vaccination, grooming, boarding, counter sales, cashier, inventory, customer care. Monorepo: `BE/` (Java 21, Spring Boot 3.5, Maven, PostgreSQL 17 + Flyway) and `FE/` (React 18 + Vite + TypeScript). Redis 7 is configured but unused; SMTP is used by the notification worker (dev: Mailpit). Documentation is Vietnamese; code and identifiers are English.

New debt D026 (branch, catalog). **T17 customer & pets (2026-10-16, branch `dung` @ `7f19bad` + 6 local commits, not pushed, ADR-0041, BE-2 work):** customer-v1 #8–21 — `GET/POST /api/customers`, `GET/PATCH /api/customers/{id}`, `POST /api/customers/{id}/pets` (RECEPTIONIST writes; RECEPTIONIST / VET / CARETAKER read; ADMIN / SUPER_MANAGER / BRANCH_MANAGER have **no** access), `GET/POST /api/me/pets`, `GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/pets/{id}`, `POST …/mark-deceased`, `POST …/transfer-owner`, `GET/POST …/weights`. `CustomerApi` / `CustomerQueryApi` / `PetApi` / `PetQueryApi` are real (one bean per interface: `CustomerApiService`, `CustomerQueryService`, `PetWeightApiService`, `PetQueryService`; the 3 customer placeholders are deleted → D001, D010 paid, customer register / login / `GET /api/me` / `/api/me/link-*` work). Lock order `accounts → customers (id ↑) → pets → weight_records`; every pet write locks the pet row first (`findByIdForUpdate`), then asks visit / boarding / sales; a module that inserts a row pointing at a pet (visit check-in, appointment, boarding) **must** call `PetQueryApi.findPetForShare` first (`FOR SHARE`, ADR-0041 §3 — `PetLifecycleIT` races + mutation prove it). A customer sees only own pets, anything else (incl. a former owner) is 404 with the same message as a missing id; a pending link decision (BR-TK-19) gives 400 on every pet operation of the customer (contract fixed in `m_customer.py`). Search (#8) is `NamedParameterJdbcTemplate` that appends only the sent conditions, email exact lower-case, name `ILIKE` accent-sensitive; `maskedEmail` / candidate names are masked by `customer/service/Maskers`. Dead pet: second mark = 409, edit / delete / transfer / weight = 400 BR-KH-05. Transfer writes audit `PET_OWNER_TRANSFERRED` and publishes events in the same transaction (no listener exists yet). `createOnlineProfile` has no email parameter → online profile `customers.email` is null. Cross-module reads were implemented **partially in the existing `*Placeholder` classes** of visit / appointment / boarding / sales (other methods still throw) and `content/service/FeedbackQueryService` is new; owners replace them. `V13__pet_customer_fk_indexes.sql` (6 FK indexes, D013 paid; number not yet confirmed with BE-1). `OpenApiContractParityIT` now counts 59 endpoints. `mvn clean verify` 2026-10-16: 3188 unit + 1035 IT green (before T17: 3092 + 942); jar + Mailpit smoke 37/37 (customer register → verify → login → UC07 link by OTP → pets / weights / transfer / deceased / audit). Debts are in the local `docs/dept` (gitignored). **T18 inventory (2026-10-16, branch `dung`, 7 local commits after `6028bd7`, not pushed, ADR-0042 + 0043, BE-2 work):** inventory-v1 #1–17 — `/api/suppliers` (BRANCH_MANAGER, chain-wide), `/api/stock-receipts` (+ `confirm`, `cancel`; FSM 03 #9; BRANCH_MANAGER of own branch, other branch 403), `/api/inventory` (+ `lots`, `min-quantity`; view by A05–A08, lots A05/A06, write A05), `/api/stock-adjustments`. `StockApi` / `StockQueryApi` are real (`StockIssueService`, `StockQueryService`; `StockQueryApiPlaceholder` deleted → turning off `tracksExpiry` is 400 BR-SP-05, no longer 500). **Every change of `stock_lots.quantity` goes through `StockLedger`** (never negative / overflow, always one `stock_movements` row) and every flow locks all lots it touches **in one statement ordered by lot id** (`StockLotDao.lockLots*`, `lockIssuableLots`) — no `LIMIT 1 … FOR UPDATE`; expired = `expiry_date < today` (Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh), FEFO never issues expired lots; `issueForSale` returns `List<Shortage>` and deducts nothing when anything is short. Sales / visit must lock their own document first and then call `StockApi` (none does yet). Receipt codes `PN-yyMM-nnnnn`, adjustments `DC-…` come from `platform/sequence/DocumentCodeGenerator` (table `document_counters`, V14, gap-free, ADR-0043) — reuse it for other documents. Confirming a receipt reads products via `CatalogQueryApi.findProductForShare`; `ProductService.updateProduct` now locks the row first. Inventory list JOINs `products` read-only (deliberate boundary exception, ADR-0042 §6). Audit: `STOCK_RECEIPT_CANCELLED` (confirmed receipts only), `STOCK_ADJUSTED`. `OpenApiContractParityIT` counts 76 endpoints. `StockReportApi` / ST08 left for T44. `mvn clean verify` 2026-10-16: 3232 unit + 1117 IT green (before: 3188 + 1035); jar + docker smoke 27/27. Debts in the local `docs/dept/T18-kho.md` (gitignored). **T19 Order core (2026-10-17, branch `dung`, 5 local commits after `ec9c6fd`, not pushed, ADR-0044, BE-2 work):** sales-v1 #3–6 — `GET /api/orders/{orderId}` (A05–A08 of the order's branch), `POST /api/orders/{orderId}/lines` (RECEPTIONIST → GOODS, VET → SERVICE group MEDICAL, CARETAKER → SERVICE group GROOMING; VET / CARETAKER must be the visit's `assignee_id`; OPEN takes every line, PENDING only GOODS), `PATCH` / `DELETE …/lines/{lineId}`. `SalesOrder` (table `orders`, not `Order`: JPQL keyword) + `OrderLine`; `totalAmount` is **recomputed from the lines** (`addExact`; overflow = 400 `VALIDATION_FAILED`); price + name snapshotted when the line is added (BR-BH-03, product read `FOR SHARE`). FSM `OrderTransitionHandler` has the 10 transitions of 03 #5 — source-specific conditions are service guards (`cancelByVisit` requires OPEN). **Every write locks the `orders` row first** (`findByIdForUpdate` / `findByVisitIdForUpdate`; a line is found by an unlocked read of its order id, then locked, then re-read); the REST layer never locks `visits` (reads `VisitQueryApi.findVisit` unlocked), so the order is `visits → orders → products → stock lots`. Line permissions live in the pure `OrderLinePolicy` and feed both the guards and the `canEdit` / `canDelete` flags: VACCINE / BOARDING never edited directly (400 BR-BH-02), DRUG only through the prescription, SERVICE only by `owner_account_id` and locked when PENDING (403), GOODS by any receptionist; 403 carries `BR-BH-02` via `OrderLineAccessDeniedException`. PATCH only lowers quantity; `serviceId` only on the auto-generated SERVICE line of a VISIT order (same group as the role, price re-snapshotted). Order codes `DH-yyMM-nnnnn` from `DocumentCodeGenerator`. `VisitOrderApi` (10 methods, `MANDATORY`) and `OrderQueryApi` are real (`OrderQueryApiPlaceholder` deleted); `VisitQueryApiPlaceholder.findVisit` reads `visits` with SQL. **Obligation for visit (BE-1):** record an injection = `addVaccineLine` → `lockFefoLot` → INSERT `vaccinations` → `issueForVaccination`; delete one = delete `vaccinations` → `removeVaccineLine` → `revertVaccination` (06 §3 corrected). Not built: sales-v1 #1 #2 #7 #8 #10 (T29), payments / shifts #11–21 (T30), `abort-checkout` + `BoardingOrderApi` (T43). `V15__order_lines_index.sql`. `OpenApiContractParityIT` counts 80 endpoints. `mvn clean verify` 2026-10-17: 3899 unit + 1165 IT green (before: 3232 + 1117); jar + docker smoke 35/35 (xem, thêm / sửa / xóa dòng theo từng vai trò, PENDING / CANCELLED, Swagger). Debts in the local `docs/dept/T19-order.md` (gitignored). **Swagger docs (uncommitted, 2026-10-14, ADR-0040):** springdoc annotations on the 45 implemented endpoints of identity, care, customer (`@Operation` text copied from the contract, `@ApiErrorDoc`, `@Parameter`, `@Schema` examples on 43 DTOs + `ApiResponse` / `PageResponse` / `ErrorResponse`); `OpenApiConfig` rewritten (scheme `bearer-jwt` → `bearerAuth`, shared error responses, tags), `ApiErrorDocCustomizer`, `PublicPathSecurityCustomizer`, `SecurityConfig.publicPaths()`, `springdoc.override-with-generic-response: false`; no runtime behaviour change. Baseline before the change: 3086 unit + 931 IT (`26158e6` + docs, `mvn clean verify` 2026-10-14); after it: 3092 unit + 942 IT green; docker-compose smoke (spec 3.1 with 82 operations, login public, `lockAccount` 404 `$ref`, 401 + 500 on `/api/me`, Swagger UI and `/v3/api-docs` anonymous even with a garbage token, ADMIN by SQL → login → `GET /api/me` 200, 401 envelope = `ErrorResponse` schema) 13/13, live spec passes `openapi-spec-validator`. New debt D026 (branch, catalog). **T15 part 5 (2026-10-14, docs only):** notification settings (care-v1 #9–10, UC88) split off from T15 — PO has not decided the content (care-v1 Q1) and no consumer (ST03 T25, ST04 T39) exists yet → debt **D025**, built in the same PR as ST03 (at the latest ST04); `accounts.notification_settings` stays `'{}'`, every reminder follows BR-TB-02. **T15 part 4 (committed `26158e6`, 2026-10-14, ADR-0039):** in-app notifications of the signed-in user, care-v1 #5 `GET /api/me/notifications` (`unreadOnly?`, `page`, `size`), #6 `GET …/unread-count`, #7 `POST …/{notificationId}/read`, #8 `POST …/read-all` (UC88 read part; #9–10 settings not built — care-v1 Q1 TBD). `care/controller/MyNotificationController` (`@PreAuthorize("isAuthenticated()")` on every handler, **not** exempt from BR-TK-17) → `care/service/MyNotificationService` (owner always `BranchScope.current().accountId()`, no body, no account parameter; reads `readOnly` at default READ COMMITTED — count and page may differ by one row while ST20 delivers, unlike ADR-0038; writes plain `@Transactional`, no audit / outbox / event). `NotificationRepository` native queries with constant conditions (two read methods split by `unreadOnly`, never `:x IS NULL OR`), `ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC`, page/size clamped like #28. #7: one `UPDATE … SET read_at = :now, updated_at = :now WHERE id = :id AND account_id = :accountId AND read_at IS NULL`; only when 0 rows → `existsByIdAndAccountId` → already read 204 (keeps the first `readAt`), missing **or someone else's** → 404 "Không tìm thấy thông báo #<id>". #8: same UPDATE by `account_id`; rows ST20 commits after the statement snapshot stay unread. Bulk UPDATEs set `updated_at` themselves (ADR-0015). Response `NotificationResponse` (7 contract keys, MapStruct `NotificationMapper`, no `accountId` / `updatedAt`), `UnreadCountResponse(int count)`. Index V1 `ix_notifications_account_read_created`: counts and UPDATEs use it fully; both page queries still Sort the account's rows (EXPLAIN 2026-10-14) — debt **D022**; no cleanup of `notifications` — **D023**; any authenticated staff request (incl. polling) refreshes `last_seen_at`, so a polling tab keeps staff "online" (BR-TN-06) — **D024**. No migration. Tests: `MyNotificationServiceTest`, `NotificationMapperTest`, `MyNotificationControllerSecurityTest`, `module/care/MyNotificationIT` (25 cases; own accounts per test — `InAppNotificationIT` deletes the whole table; row-lock wait proves #7/#8 keep a concurrent first `readAt`; EXPLAIN in a rolled-back transaction). **T15 part 3 (committed `38ed27e`, 2026-10-14, ADR-0038):** `GET /api/audit-logs` (#36, UC11), `@PreAuthorize` ADMIN only (`AuditLogController`, GET only — other methods 405; 403 not audited; viewing is **not** audited, Q5). `AuditLogQueryService.listAuditLogs` `@Transactional(readOnly = true, isolation = REPEATABLE_READ)` — first non-default isolation in the repo, so count and page share one snapshot on an insert-heavy table (`BEGIN READ ONLY`; Spring/Hikari reset the connection, ADR-0020 L22). Read path `identity/repository/AuditLogQueryRepository` = `NamedParameterJdbcTemplate` + builder `AuditLogQuery` that only appends conditions actually sent (**never** `:x IS NULL OR` — generic plans lose the index), 11 columns, `ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT/OFFSET` (deliberate deviation from convention 02). Filters exact match, closed `[from, to]` (ISO with offset, `+` must be `%2B`), blank `action`/`entityType` = not sent, unknown value → empty page (never 404), page/size clamped like #28. Order: wrong type → 400 `MALFORMED_REQUEST`, `action`/`entityType` > 50 → 400 `VALIDATION_FAILED` (both before `@PreAuthorize`) → 403 → R1 `from > to` (on `to`), R2 `entityId` without `entityType` (on `entityType`), R3 `action` without `actorAccountId` or `entityType + entityId` needs `from` + `to` ≤ 31 days (on `from`, `AuditLogFilter.MAX_ACTION_WINDOW`, not [CFG]) → 400 `VALIDATION_FAILED` via class-level `@ValidAuditLogFilter` + `Validator` in the service, fixed R1→R2→R3 order (private `ConstraintViolationException` subclass: the stock constructor copies into a `HashSet`); not BR codes. Index map proven by EXPLAIN: actor → `ix_audit_logs_actor_created_at`, entity → `ix_audit_logs_entity`, range / none / action+range → `ix_audit_logs_created_at`. Response `AuditLogEntryResponse` (11 keys, no join: `actorEmail` as recorded, `actorAccountId` kept for deleted accounts; `beforeData`/`afterData` raw jsonb via `@JsonRawValue`; `createdAt` `Instant`). `AuditRecorder.snapshot` now rejects snapshots that are not a JSON object (List / String / number → `IllegalArgumentException`, both `record` and `recordIndependently`). New debts **D020** (exact count + OFFSET), **D021** (no index on `action`). No migration. Tests: `AuditLogFilterValidationTest`, `AuditLogQueryTest`, `AuditLogQueryServiceTest`, `AuditLogMapperTest`, `AuditLogControllerSecurityTest`, `AuditRecorderTest` (+2), `module/identity/AuditLogViewIT` (31 cases; audit rows in year 2003, one 2-day window and one `actor_account_id` range per test; EXPLAIN on 60 000 rows inserted in a rolled-back transaction; mutations dropping the isolation or `readOnly` turn it red). Baseline before the change: 3037 unit + 875 IT (`5bf7961`, run 2026-10-13); after it (`mvn clean verify` 2026-10-14): 3067 unit + 906 IT green, docker-compose smoke (ADMIN + SUPER_MANAGER by SQL → login → own `LOGIN_SUCCEEDED` row → PUT [CFG] → `SYSTEM_CONFIG_UPDATED` with object before/after → `%2B` range → R1/R2/R3/malformed 400 → 403/401/405 → GET writes no audit → size clamp) 25/25. **T15 part 2 (committed `5bf7961`, 2026-10-13, ADR-0037):** `GET /api/notification-templates` (#33), `PUT /api/notification-templates/{code}` (#34), `POST …/{code}/restore-default` (#35), `@PreAuthorize` ADMIN only (`NotificationTemplateController`, 403 not audited). No facade: `NotificationTemplateAdminService` (one `@Transactional` per write, no `noRollbackFor`, catches nothing; list readOnly sorted by `code`). Order #34: Bean Validation (`UpdateTemplateRequest`: `subject` `@NotBlank @Size(max = 255)` for **every** channel — contract changed `subject?` → `subject`; `body` `@NotBlank`, no length limit → D018) → `NotificationTemplateRepository.findByCodeForUpdate` (`FOR NO KEY UPDATE`, before any other read of the row) → 404 "Không tìm thấy mẫu thông báo #<code>" → `TemplateVariables.scan` (package-private, strict grammar: braces only as `{name}` with name ∈ `allowed_vars`, case-sensitive; unknown token, stray `{`/`}`, missing `required_vars` over subject ∪ body) → 400 BR-QT-14 naming **every** bad variable, one message → content identical (verbatim, no strip / CRLF normalisation) → no UPDATE but **still audited** before = after (BR-QT-15, unlike ADR-0036) → `NotificationTemplate.changeContent` → `flush()` → audit `NOTIFICATION_TEMPLATE_UPDATED` (`entity = notification_templates`, `entityId` null, before/after `{code, subject, body}`). #35 same with `restoreDefault`, audit `NOTIFICATION_TEMPLATE_RESTORED`, defaults not re-scanned (IT proves every seeded default passes). Response `NotificationTemplateResponse` (7 contract keys, `channel` = `NotificationApi.Channel`, `@JsonProperty("isDefault")`, `isDefault` = character-exact equality with `default_*`). The ST20 renderer (`care/service/NotificationTemplateRenderer`) is untouched; scanner ↔ renderer kept in sync by `NotificationTemplateSyntaxParityTest`; renderer substitutes sequentially → new debt D019; no newline ban in subject (Jakarta Mail folds lines — `NotificationDispatchServiceTest.lineBreakInSubjectCannotInjectHeaders`). Edits apply from the next read after commit, including outbox rows queued before. No migration. Tests: `TemplateVariablesTest`, `NotificationTemplateSyntaxParityTest`, `NotificationTemplateAdminServiceTest`, `NotificationTemplateTest`, `NotificationTemplateAdminMapperTest`, `UpdateTemplateRequestValidationTest`, `module/identity/NotificationTemplateAdminIT` (32 cases; `@AfterEach` restores every template to its defaults). Baseline before the change (`04bb155`, `mvn clean verify` 2026-10-13): 2970 unit + 843 IT green; after it: 3037 unit + 875 IT green, docker-compose smoke (ADMIN by SQL → #33 → 403 for SUPER_MANAGER → BR-QT-14 / 400 / 404 → valid PUT → forgot password → Mailpit shows the edited subject and body → #35 twice → audits UPDATED + 2 × RESTORED, the second before = after) 19/19. **T15 part 1 (committed `04bb155`, 2026-10-13, ADR-0036):** `GET /api/system-configs` (#31) and `PUT /api/system-configs/{key}` (#32), `@PreAuthorize` ADMIN only (`SystemConfigController`, 403 not audited). Facade `SystemConfigAdminService` (**not** `@Transactional`) → `SystemConfigEditService.updateConfig` (one `@Transactional`, no `noRollbackFor`, catches nothing) → then `SystemConfigService.reload()` (now `synchronized`; replaces ADR-0004's `afterCommit` idea — DB reads in `afterCommit` still join the committed tx). Order: key not in `ConfigKey` → 404 "Không tìm thấy tham số #<key>" before any DB access → `SystemConfigRepository.findByKeyForUpdate` (`FOR NO KEY UPDATE`) for the key **and its cross-key partner, ascending key, before any other read of those rows** (stale persistence-context trap, ADR-0029 §4) → `ConfigValues.parseRequest` (shared with `load`; TIME `HH:mm` only, DECIMAL no exponent) + locked row's min–max + `ConfigConstraints` (`visit.late_priority_minutes < appointment.no_show_after_minutes`, `boarding.min_nights ≤ boarding.max_nights`; also checked by `load` at startup) → 400 BR-QT-13 whose message shows the **effective** allowed range (wrong type is BR-QT-13 too) → same canonical value → 200, no write, no audit (still reloads) → `SystemConfig.changeValue(canonical, actor)` → `flush()` → audit `SYSTEM_CONFIG_UPDATED` (`entity = system_configs`, `entityId` null, before/after `{key, value}`). Reload failing after commit → log `SYSTEM_CONFIG_RELOAD_FAILED`, 500; retrying the same value heals the cache. Live-read keys (`otp.max_failed_attempts`, `login.*`…) apply to work in progress by design (ADR-0036 §9). New debt **D018** (no request body size limit, app-wide). No migration. Tests: `ConfigValuesTest`, `ConfigConstraintsTest`, `SystemConfigServiceTest` (fixture: `no_show` = 6; cross-key, 1000 unknown rows, overlapping reloads), `SystemConfigEditServiceTest`, `SystemConfigAdminServiceTest`, `SystemConfigAdminMapperTest`, `UpdateConfigRequestValidationTest`, `module/identity/SystemConfigAdminIT` (73 cases: contract shape vs SQL row by row, role matrix, BR-TK-17, every `ConfigKey` boundary, cross-key messages, malformed bodies, 405, failing triggers on `audit_logs` INSERT / `system_configs` UPDATE, reload failure via `@MockitoSpyBean SystemConfigRepository`, pair race with a `flush()` barrier — removing the partner lock leaves both commits and `reload()` then 500s — and same-key serialisation; `@AfterEach` restores the V2 values + `reload()`). **T14 part 5 (committed `6ed0962`, 2026-10-12, ADR-0035):** `GET /api/accounts` (#28), `POST /api/accounts/{id}/lock` (#29, Tài khoản#7), `/unlock` (#30, #8), `@PreAuthorize` ADMIN only (`AccountAdminController`; not through `StaffHierarchyPolicy`, wrong-role 403 not audited). #28 `AccountAdminQueryService` (readOnly, projection `AccountSummaryRow`, `q` in email with `StaffQueryService.likePattern`, `role` / `isLocked` filters, size 1–100, every role and status). Lock `AccountLockService.lockAccount` (one `@Transactional`, no facade, no `noRollbackFor`, catches nothing): self → 400 BR-QT-11 before any read → `findRoleById` projection (404) → if BRANCH_MANAGER `BranchApi.lockForStaffChange({branch read unlocked})` → `findByIdForUpdate` (404, e.g. ST02 deleted it meanwhile) → profile → BRANCH_MANAGER whose current branch is not in the lock map → 409 `ConcurrencyConflictException` → already locked → 409 → **read** pending work and recipients (`PendingWorkCollector`: A05–A08 any status → `VisitQueryApi.findUnfinishedVisitsAssignedTo` + `CashierShiftQueryApi.findOpenShift`; customer → `CustomerQueryApi` → `AppointmentQueryApi.findBookedByCustomer`, `BoardingQueryApi.findActiveStaysByCustomer`, `OrderQueryApi.findUnpaidOrdersByCustomer`; ADMIN / SUPER_MANAGER → none; a placeholder throws here → 500 with nothing written) → **write** `Account.lock(reason)` (`is_locked`, `locked_reason`, `last_seen_at = NULL`; `status` kept) → `revokeAll` → `publishEvent(AccountLockedEvent)` (no listener yet — D017, T26) → IN_APP `enqueue` (`AccountLockNotices`: A06–A08 → active BRANCH_MANAGERs of the branch, none → every SUPER_MANAGER, always sent; BRANCH_MANAGER → every SUPER_MANAGER, the **last** active manager of an `ACTIVE` branch read under the branch lock → one `LAST_BRANCH_MANAGER_LOCKED_APP` instead; customer → active receptionists of each branch with pending work, only that branch's items, none → WARN `ACCOUNT_LOCK_NO_RECIPIENT`; payload carries the reason) → audit `ACCOUNT_LOCKED` (before/after `{isLocked, status}`, `reason`) → flush → `LockResult{account, pendingWork}` sorted by enum order then `entityId` (`VISIT_IN_PROGRESS` = `needsReassign`). Unlock `unlockAccount`: `findByIdForUpdate` → not locked → 409 → `Account.unlock()` (only `is_locked`; `status` and `locked_reason` kept, BR-QT-12) → audit `ACCOUNT_UNLOCKED`; no branch lock, no session, no notice. `ReasonRequest.reason` `@NotBlank @Size(max = 500)`, stripped. Contract change for BE-2: `VisitQueryApi.findUnfinishedVisitIdsAssignedTo` → `findUnfinishedVisitsAssignedTo` (`AssignedVisit(visitId, branchId, status)`), `CashierShiftQueryApi.hasOpenShift` → `findOpenShift` (`Optional<OpenShift(shiftId, branchId)>`), `StaffAccountService.checkNoPendingWork` uses them (BR-QT-08 message unchanged); new `sales/service/OrderQueryApiPlaceholder` (no `OrderQueryApi` bean existed). `V12__seed_account_lock_templates.sql` (`ACCOUNT_LOCKED_FOLLOW_UP_APP`, `LAST_BRANCH_MANAGER_LOCKED_APP`). On the real app: lock / unlock ADMIN and SUPER_MANAGER, unlock of anyone and #28 work; locking A05–A08 (D016) or a customer (D010 + appointment / boarding / order placeholders) is 500 with nothing written. **T14 part 4 (committed `0f27381`, 2026-10-12, ADR-0034):** `POST /api/staff/{id}/email-change` (#23) and `/email-change/confirm` (#24), `@PreAuthorize` ADMIN / SUPER_MANAGER / BRANCH_MANAGER, facade `StaffAdminService.{startEmailChange, confirmEmailChange}` → **new bean** `StaffEmailChangeService` (`StaffAccountService` untouched: 3 unit tests build it with `new`). #23: `OtpService.prepare()` outside the tx → `issueEmailChangeOtp` (one `@Transactional`): `findByIdForUpdate` (404 missing / customer) → profile → policy `CHANGE_EMAIL` (403 audited after rollback) → normalize lower-case → same as current email → 400 BR-TK-01 "Email mới trùng email hiện tại" (Q5) → `existsByEmail` → 400 BR-TK-01 → `issuePrepared(CHANGE_EMAIL)` (BR-TK-07; invalidates the **account's** previous code — `OtpPurpose.invalidatesPerAccount()` is true for LINK_PROFILE and CHANGE_EMAIL, replaces the `customerId != null` test; `issueOtp` untouched) → outbox `OTP_EMAIL_CHANGE` to the new email; 200 `OtpSentResponse` (`maskedEmail` null); no audit (Q4). #24 (body only `{code}`): readOnly `findEmailChangeCode` (`findStaffRow` 404 → policy 403 **before** looking up the code → `OtpService.findActiveForAccount(id, CHANGE_EMAIL)` — new, CHANGE_EMAIL only, returns `AccountBoundOtp(id, targetEmail, codeHash)`; empty → facade 400 BR-TK-05) → BCrypt `matches` outside any tx → `applyEmailChange` (`noRollbackFor = OtpRejectedException`): lock `accounts` → profile → **policy re-checked under the lock** → `settleCheckedOtp` first write → `existsByEmail` (BR-TK-01 → rollback incl. `consumed_at`) → `Account.changeEmail` (only `email`) → `EMAIL_CHANGED_NOTICE` to old **and** new email → audit `STAFF_EMAIL_CHANGED` (before `{email}`, after `{email, verificationMethod: EMAIL_CODE}`) → `accounts.flush()` (`uq_accounts_email` → 409) → re-read projection. Any status (Q1), no session revoke (Q2), no `branches` lock. Accepted race R14: a login with the old email already past its unlocked read may succeed once. Debt D015 extended (quota per target email, unlocked count across accounts, Q7). No migration. **T14 part 3 (committed `c103a3b`, 2026-10-12, ADR-0033):** `POST /api/staff/{id}/disable` (#20, Tài khoản#5) and `/reactivate` (#21, #6), `@PreAuthorize` ADMIN / SUPER_MANAGER / BRANCH_MANAGER, same facade. Disable (`StaffAccountService.disableStaff`, one `@Transactional`): `lockTarget` (branch → `accounts` → profile) → policy `DISABLE` (403 audited) → A05–A08 without branch → 500 → BR-QT-12 (`is_locked`, any status) → **only if `ACTIVE` and A05–A08**: branch moved 409 → BR-QT-04 → BR-QT-08 last (SUPER_MANAGER never asked — Q2; placeholder → 500, D016) → `validateTransition` (**already DISABLED → 409**, Q1) → `Account.disable()` (`last_seen_at = NULL`) → `revokeAll` → audit `STAFF_DISABLED` (`{status}`) → flush → re-read; no email (Q3). Reactivate (`reactivateStaff`): facade `prepare()` temp password outside tx → `findByIdForUpdate` (404; **no `branches` lock** — only raises the manager count) → policy `REACTIVATE` (BM sending another branch → 403) → BR-QT-10 (`is_locked`) → BR-QT-03 (SUPER_MANAGER with `branchId`, unknown branch via `BranchQueryApi.findBranch`) → `validateTransition` (ACTIVE → 409) → `Account.reactivate(hash)` (ACTIVE, `must_change_password`, counters / `locked_until` / `last_seen_at` cleared) → optional `transferTo` → `revokeAll` → outbox `STAFF_TEMP_PASSWORD` → audit `STAFF_REACTIVATED` (`{status, branchId}`) → flush → re-read. Body of #21 optional (`@RequestBody(required = false)`; null / `{}` keeps the branch). `checkNoPendingWork(id, action)` now takes the message prefix. `AccountTransitionHandler.fsmName()` = "tài khoản" (409 message no longer leaks the class name). **D014 paid** (every flow lowering the ACTIVE BRANCH_MANAGER count locks the branch first; mutation removing it from disable → 5/5 race ITs red). No migration. **T14 part 2 (committed `b55b906`, 2026-10-12, ADR-0031):** `POST /api/staff/{id}/change-role` (#18) and `/transfer` (#19), `@PreAuthorize("hasRole('SUPER_MANAGER')")`, through the same facade (`StaffAdminService`, no tx, audits 403 after rollback) → `StaffAccountService.changeStaffRole` / `transferStaff` (one `@Transactional`, no `noRollbackFor`): `lockTarget` = `findStaffRow` projection (missing / customer → 404) → `BranchApi.lockForStaffChange({current branch} ∪ {destination})` → `findByIdForUpdate(accounts)` → read `staff_profiles` → policy (403 audited) → A05–A08 without branch → 500 → **same value → 200, no write, no audit** (checked before the conflict check, so a double-click that already happened while waiting gets 200) → branch changed while waiting → 409 `ConcurrencyConflictException` → (#19) destination absent from the lock map → 400 BR-QT-03 → BR-QT-04 (target BRANCH_MANAGER `ACTIVE` + branch read under lock `ACTIVE` + `countActiveBranchManagers ≤ 1`; source branch for #19) → (#19) BR-QT-08 **last**, both `VisitQueryApi` and `CashierShiftQueryApi` for every role, message lists everything (placeholders → transfer 500 until 21/10, D016) → `Account.changeStaffRole` (A05–A08 only) [+ `StaffProfile.clearVetProfile()` when leaving VET] / `StaffProfile.transferTo` → audit `STAFF_ROLE_CHANGED` (`before`/`after` = `role, specialty, bio`, `LinkedHashMap` because values may be null) / `STAFF_TRANSFERRED` (`branchId`) → `flush()` → re-read `findStaffDetail`. User decisions: DISABLED / locked targets allowed (Q1), same value no-op (Q2), VET leaving VET clears specialty/bio (Q3), change-role does **not** check pending work (Q4, TBD before T26); no session revoke, no email. Obligation (ADR-0031 §9, 06 §4, D016): assigning a visit (T26) / opening a cashier shift (BE-2) must `SELECT … FROM accounts WHERE id = <staff> FOR SHARE` and re-check branch/role/status under it — FK checks only take `FOR KEY SHARE`, which does not conflict with transfer's `FOR NO KEY UPDATE`. **ADR-0032 (same day):** `spring.jackson.deserialization.accept-float-as-int: false` in `application.yml` — `{"branchId": 1.9}` used to become `1` silently; now any float into an integer field is 400 `MALFORMED_REQUEST` (whole app, BE-2 told via 06 §8 Q5; `BigDecimal`/`double` unaffected). Contract #18/#19 gained 409 + notes (generator). No migration. **T14 part 1 (committed `90dfc6c`, 2026-10-11, ADR-0030 — first way to create staff without SQL):** `StaffController` `/api/staff` (identity-v1 #15 list, #16 create, #17 get, #22 `POST {id}/resend-initial-password`; `@PreAuthorize` ADMIN / SUPER_MANAGER / BRANCH_MANAGER). Create/resend go through facade `StaffAdminService` (**not** `@Transactional`: `TemporaryPasswordGenerator.prepare()` BCrypt outside the tx; catches `StaffHierarchyDeniedException` after rollback → `recordIndependently` → rethrow) → `StaffAccountService` (one `@Transactional`). Create: policy → BR-QT-03 (SUPER_MANAGER with `branchId` → 400; A05–A08 without branch / unknown branch → 400; BRANCH_MANAGER null = own branch; no `branches` lock) → BR-TK-01 → `validateInitial(ACTIVE)` → `Account.createStaff` + `StaffProfile.create` (`save` merges: PK assigned) → outbox `STAFF_TEMP_PASSWORD` `{ho_ten, email_dang_nhap, mat_khau_tam}` → audit `STAFF_CREATED` (`after` = email, phone, fullName, role, branchId, status — **no key containing `password`**, `AuditRecorder` splits camelCase and rejects it) → `flush()` → re-read projection. Request `role` is a `String` + `@Pattern` (so ADMIN/CUSTOMER/garbage = 400 VALIDATION_FAILED on field `role`). Resend: `findByIdForUpdate` first → 404 (missing / customer) → read `staff_profiles` → policy (self denied) → 409 `InvalidStateTransitionException` if `DISABLED` or `must_change_password = false` (`is_locked` allowed) → `Account.reissueInitialPassword` (hash; clears counter, `locked_until`, `last_seen_at`) → `SessionService.revokeAll` → outbox → audit `STAFF_INITIAL_PASSWORD_RESENT`, 204. View (`StaffQueryService`, readOnly, 403 not audited): ADMIN sees SUPER_MANAGER, SUPER_MANAGER sees A05–A08, BRANCH_MANAGER sees A06–A08 of own branch; `role` filter outside scope → empty page; BRANCH_MANAGER other `branchId` → 403; get: missing/customer → 404 same message. `StaffResponse` (12 contract fields, `isLocked` = `is_locked` only) built only by `StaffAdminMapper` from projection `StaffDetailRow` (secrets never SELECTed); `online` via package-private `StaffPresence` (same formula as `StaffDirectoryService`, which was left untouched; `StaffPresenceParityTest`). Side effects by design: a BRANCH_MANAGER created by API makes the branch activatable; a new VET shows on `/api/public/vets` immediately. Obligation for parts 3/5: set `last_seen_at = NULL` when revoking sessions. No migration. **T14 part 0 (committed `90dfc6c`, 2026-10-11, ADR-0029 — staff-administration foundation, no endpoint):** `identity/service/StaffHierarchyPolicy` (pure, not `@Transactional`; ADMIN → SUPER_MANAGER, SUPER_MANAGER → A05–A08, BRANCH_MANAGER → A06–A08 of own branch, `branchId` null on create = own branch; change-role/transfer SUPER_MANAGER only, also `@PreAuthorize`; self always denied) + `StaffAction`; denial throws `identity/exception/StaffHierarchyDeniedException extends AccessDeniedScopeException` (403 generic message) carrying `AuditEntry` `STAFF_ACTION_DENIED` (`QtAuditActions`) — the facade of parts 1–5 must `recordIndependently` it **after** the business tx rolled back; check order 400 → 403 role → 404 → 403 hierarchy → 400 BR → 409; lock order `branches (id ↑) → accounts → staff_profiles → sessions`, every read before a lock is a projection; `branch/api/BranchApi.lockForStaffChange` + `branch/service/BranchStaffLockService` (BE-1 in BE-2's module: `MANDATORY`, rejects `readOnly`, native scalar `SELECT status … FOR NO KEY UPDATE` via `BranchRepository.lockStatusForStaffChange` — `findByIdForUpdate` would return a stale `Branch` already loaded by `BranchQueryApi.findBranch`; ids locked ascending; unknown ids absent; D014 step 1); `identity/service/TemporaryPasswordGenerator.prepare()` (outside tx, `max(12, password.min_length)` chars, letter + digit, no `0 O o 1 l I`, record components `plainPassword`/`passwordHash` so `AuditRecorder` rejects it); `visit/service/VisitQueryApiPlaceholder`, `sales/service/CashierShiftQueryApiPlaceholder` (debt D016: transfer/disable will 500 until 21/10); `V11__seed_staff_admin_templates.sql` (`STAFF_TEMP_PASSWORD`, `OTP_EMAIL_CHANGE`, `EMAIL_CHANGED_NOTICE`; **V11 confirmed with BE-2 on 2026-10-12**). Earlier, **task 08 part 4 (committed `ec8b639`, 2026-10-10, ADR-0028 — BE-1 builds in BE-2's `customer` module):** UC06 for customers, customer-v1 #1–7: `GET/PATCH /api/me/customer-profile` and `/api/me/addresses` (list, add 201, patch, delete 204, `POST {id}/set-default`) in `module/customer` — first entities of that module, `Customer` (every `customers` column) + `Address` (`is_default` ↔ field `defaultAddress`, `@JsonProperty("isDefault")` on the record), `MyCustomerController` (CUSTOMER only, not blocked by the BR-TK-19 pending flag), `MyCustomerProfileService`, `AddressBookService`, `MyCustomerLookup`; every write first locks the owner's `customers` row (`FOR NO KEY UPDATE` by `account_id`; lock order `customers → addresses`, never `accounts`; if the lock query finds no row — UC07 replaced the profile while it waited — re-read the id with a new statement and lock by id); BR-TK-18 limit `address.max_per_customer` [CFG] counted under that lock; first address always default; `AddressRepository.clearDefault` (native bulk UPDATE, `flushAutomatically`, sets `updated_at`) runs **before** the INSERT/UPDATE of the new default because Hibernate flushes INSERT before UPDATE and `uq_addresses_default_per_customer` is not deferrable; deleting the default while others exist → 400 BR-TK-18; hard delete of addresses (erd §0 now lists BR-TK-18); someone else's address → 404 same message; PATCH profile: `email` sent → 400 BR-TK-15 before any read, null keeps / blank clears, COUNTER profile clearing phone → 400 BR-KH-01; response `email` = the **account** email from the principal (not `customers.email`); request phones `^0\d{9}$`, avatar `https://` only, response keeps the generic types; Bean Validation runs before `@PreAuthorize` (staff with a bad body gets 400); no audit; `V10__address_customer_index.sql` (`ix_addresses_customer_id`, V10 confirmed with BE-2 2026-10-10) pays the `addresses` part of D013. `CustomerApi` / `CustomerQueryApi` stay placeholders (D001, D010). Earlier, as of 2026-10-09 (`manh` = `origin/dev` @ `fe67ded`; after PR #9 BE-2 added the `branch` module — UC12, UC14, UC33, UC42, `BranchQueryApi` real, see `docs/architecture/system-overview.md` — and FE-1 the T5 FE shell). **Task 08 part 3 (committed `c304e97`, 2026-10-10, ADR-0027 — completes ADR-0025):** UC07 profile link, identity-v1 #11–14 (`MeController` → `LinkProfileService`, **not** `@Transactional`, BCrypt outside → `LinkProfileAttemptService`; CUSTOMER only): `GET /api/me/link-candidates?phone=` (readOnly: `CustomerQueryApi.checkOnlineProfileLinkable` ≠ `LINKABLE` → 400 BR-TK-19, then `findLinkCandidates(phone or online-profile phone)`; first constrained `@RequestParam` in the repo → `HandlerMethodValidationException` 400), `POST /api/me/link/otp` (`{customerId, phone?}`: `prepare()` → `findByIdForUpdate` → BR-TK-11 → not a candidate of that phone → 404 (one message) → BR-TK-19 linkability / counter profile without email → `issuePrepared(LINK_PROFILE, counterEmail, C)` → outbox `OTP_PROFILE_LINK` to the **counter profile email** → `{resendAvailableAt, maskedEmail}`), `POST /api/me/link/confirm` (readOnly `findLinkCode` → BCrypt → `applyLink` `noRollbackFor = OtpRejectedException`: lock `accounts` → BR-TK-11 → **`settleCheckedOtp` as the first write** → `CustomerApi.linkAccountToCounterProfile(X, C, emailTheCodeWentTo, X.email)` (customer re-checks under its lock, BR-TK-19 → rollback incl. `consumed_at`) → audit `CUSTOMER_PROFILE_LINKED` written by identity), `POST /api/me/link/decline` (204; lock `accounts` → no flag → 409 `InvalidStateTransitionException`, created by the service because the flag has no 03 table). Lock order `accounts → otp_tokens → customers`. `customer.api` changed: `linkAccountToCounterProfile` signature (no `actorId`, adds `expectedCounterEmail`), new `checkOnlineProfileLinkable` + enum `OnlineProfileLinkability`; customer must delete the online profile's `addresses`, then the profile, then `flush()` before setting `C.account_id` (`uq_customers_account_id` is not deferrable, Hibernate flushes UPDATE before DELETE). Both still throwing placeholders → all 4 endpoints 500 on the real app (D001, D010); new debt D015 (link-OTP quota is per target email). No migration. **Task 08 part 2 (committed `ecb6bc2`, 2026-10-10, ADR-0026 — supersedes ADR-0024 §5):** `PATCH /api/me/staff-profile` (UC06, identity-v1 #10: `MeController.updateStaffProfile` → `StaffProfileService`, one `@Transactional`: `email` in body → 400 BR-TK-15 before any read → `findByIdForUpdate` on `accounts` **then** read `staff_profiles` (lock order `accounts → staff_profiles`; Hibernate writes every column, so reading under the lock is what stops overwriting a concurrent `branch_id`/`last_seen_at` change) → locked / not `ACTIVE` → 400 BR-TK-11 → non-VET sending `specialty`/`bio` → 403 → `bio` sent and longer than `vet.bio_max_length` [CFG] (code points) → 400 BR-TK-20 → `null` keeps, empty after `strip` clears → `flush()` → `MeMapper.toStaffProfileResponse`; staff phone `^0\d{9}$` (erd `accounts.phone`), `avatarUrl` `https://` only; no audit) and `GET /api/public/vets` (UC15, #37: `PublicVetService` readOnly filters `StaffDirectoryApi.listPublicVets()` by `BranchQueryApi.listActiveBranches()` — identity now imports `branch.api`; non-`ACTIVE`/unknown `branchId` → `[]`); contract generator gained types `mobile`, `https_url`; no migration. **Task 08 part 1 (committed `dae2acb`, 2026-10-09):** `GET /api/me` (UC06, identity-v1 #8) — `MeController.getMe` → `MeService` (`@Transactional(readOnly = true)`, role read from `accounts`; staff → `staff_profiles` + `accounts.phone`, a staff account **without** a `staff_profiles` row is bad data → `IllegalStateException` 500; customer → `CustomerQueryApi.findCustomerIdByAccountId` → `findContact(...).linkDecisionPending()`, placeholder → 500, D010) → `MeMapper` (MapStruct, reuses `LoginMapper.toAccountSummary`, `unmappedTargetPolicy = ERROR`); already exempt from BR-TK-17; no migration. **Task 08 part 0 (committed in `dae2acb`, 2026-10-09):** `StaffDirectoryApi` is real (`identity/service/StaffDirectoryService`, ADR-0024; placeholder deleted, branch activation no longer 500; `countBranchManagers` counts `status = ACTIVE` BRANCH_MANAGERs **including locked ones**; "active" = `ACTIVE` and not `is_locked`; online = `last_seen_at` within `staff.online_window_minutes` [CFG]; `listPublicVets` does not filter `ACTIVE` branches — content does), `OtpService` has `customerId` overloads for `LINK_PROFILE` (ADR-0025: lookup by account + email + customer, a new link code invalidates only the same account's link codes, guard purpose ⇔ `customerId`), `V9__otp_profile_link.sql` (template `OTP_PROFILE_LINK` + partial index `otp_tokens(customer_id)`); debts D013 (FKs to `customers` unindexed), D014 (manager count not locked — T14 must lock `branches`). Before that (`dev` @ `0d45292`): PR #8 `aa00bcf` (2026-10-07) = UC01 + UC02 + ST20 + ST02 + ST20 IN_APP + debts D004/D007/D008/D009 paid; catalog (UC28–UC31, BE-2, `bb5239d`) is on `dev`; PR #9 `0d45292` (2026-10-08) = task 07: `21866f9` = part 0 — V8, `CustomerQueryApiPlaceholder`, D010 — **part 1 — `POST /api/auth/login` + ST01, ADR-0019 Accepted, ADR-0020 OSIV off, D011** and **part 2 — `POST /api/auth/logout`, ADR-0021**; `1519500` = **part 3 — `POST /api/me/password`, ADR-0022**; `64cf1f5` = **part 4 — `POST /api/auth/password/forgot` + `/reset`, ADR-0023**):** the spec was redesigned (v16) and the backend was reset, then the shared `platform/` layer was rebuilt (see *What `platform/` already provides* below). Schema: `V1__init_schema.sql` (all 55 ERD tables) + `V2__seed_system_configs.sql` (52 [CFG] rows) + `V3__seed_notification_template_otp_register.sql` (first template, `OTP_REGISTER`) + `V4__st20_notification_outbox.sql` (outbox lane index, `OTP_REGISTER` greeting `Xin chào,`) + `V5__account_pending_expires_at.sql` (`accounts.pending_expires_at` snapshot for ST02, `ck_accounts_pending_expiry`, index `otp_tokens(account_id)`) + `V6__in_app_notification_index.sql` (partial index `ix_notification_outbox_in_app_due` for the ST20 IN_APP lane) + `V7__account_fk_indexes.sql` (38 indexes, one per FK column to `accounts` that had none usable — ADR-0018, D009 paid) + `V8__seed_notification_templates_login_password.sql` (templates `OTP_PASSWORD_RESET`, `LOGIN_LOCKED_WARNING`, `PASSWORD_CHANGED` for task 07 — ADR-0019) + `V9__otp_profile_link.sql` (template `OTP_PROFILE_LINK`, index `ix_otp_tokens_customer_id` — ADR-0025). `module/<m>/api/` holds the cross-module contracts of all 11 modules (interfaces/records/events only, `docs/06-module-contracts.md`); implemented module code: `module/identity` — session authentication, principal, BR-TK-17 interceptor, `SystemConfigApi` (ADR-0003, ADR-0004), the first scheduled job, `SessionCleanupJob` (ADR-0007, ADR-0008), and the **first business endpoints, `POST /api/auth/register`** (UC01: `AuthController` → `RegistrationService`, `OtpService`, `AccountTransitionHandler`; ADR-0009 BCrypt) **and `POST /api/auth/register/verify`** (UC02, Tài khoản#2: `RegistrationService.verifyAccount` → `OtpService.consumeOtp`; a wrong code returns 400 yet the `failed_attempts` increment commits — ADR-0010) **and `POST /api/auth/register/resend-otp`** (UC02, 2026-10-07: `RegistrationService.resendRegistrationOtp` → `OtpService.issueOtp`; payload without `ten_khach`). Every OTP flow first locks the `accounts` row with `AccountRepository.findByEmailForUpdate` (`SELECT … FOR UPDATE`, ADR-0011; D005 paid). `care` has `NotificationService` (implements `NotificationApi`, writes `notification_outbox`) and the **ST20 email worker** (2026-10-07, ADR-0012: `PriorityNotificationJob` every 5 s for `OTP_*`/`STAFF_TEMP_PASSWORD`, `NotificationOutboxJob` every 10 s for the rest, both via `NotificationOutboxRunner` → `NotificationDispatchService` — the NORMAL lane sends **batches of 20 over one SMTP connection** through `SmtpBatchSender` (ADR-0017, D008 paid); templates read through `identity.api.NotificationTemplateQueryApi`) and the **ST20 IN_APP lane** (2026-10-07, ADR-0014, D006 paid: `InAppNotificationJob` every 5 s → `InAppNotificationDeliveryService` turns each `IN_APP` outbox row into exactly one `notifications` row in the same transaction; no UC88 endpoint yet) and **`NotificationOutboxCleanupJob`** (ADR-0016, D007 paid: daily 03:40, deletes `SENT` rows older than 30 days and `FAILED` older than 90 by `created_at`, never `PENDING`). `customer` has the customer self-service endpoints of task 08 part 4 (ADR-0028) but still only `CustomerApiPlaceholder` and `CustomerQueryApiPlaceholder` for its `api/` (both throw; the latter makes customer login return 500 once login exists — debt D010, staff login unaffected), so register (and verify at its `flagLinkDecisionIfPhoneMatches` step) returns 500 on the real app until BE-2 delivers `CustomerApi` — debt D001 (resend does not call customer). **ST02** (2026-10-07, ADR-0013, D002 paid): `identity/job/PendingAccountCleanupJob` every 15 min deletes `PENDING` accounts whose `pending_expires_at` (= registration time + `account.pending_ttl_hours` [CFG], snapshotted at register, cleared by verify) has passed — one account per transaction in `PendingAccountCleanupService.purge` (`FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` + re-check → `otp_tokens` → `CustomerApi.deleteOnlineProfileOfUnverifiedAccount` → `accounts`), 60 s time budget per run, WARN `PENDING_ACCOUNT_CLEANUP_SLOW` (FK checks on `accounts` are indexed since V7); verify/resend still do not check the expiry themselves. **`POST /api/auth/login`** (UC03 + ST01, 2026-10-08, ADR-0019): `AuthController.login` → `LoginService` (**not** `@Transactional`: reads `(id, password_hash)` in a short readOnly tx, runs BCrypt while holding no connection, writes the 400-branch audit after rollback) → `LoginAttemptService.login` (`@Transactional(noRollbackFor = InvalidCredentialsException.class)`: `findByIdForUpdate`, guards temp-lock → password → PENDING/locked/DISABLED, `Account.recordFailedLogin` fixed window + `locked_until` + `LOGIN_LOCKED_WARNING` email, session, audit); every 401 has the same message (BR-TK-10); customer login reads `linkDecisionPending` via the throwing `CustomerQueryApiPlaceholder` → 500 until BE-2 (D010). Open Session In View is **off** (`spring.jpa.open-in-view: false`, ADR-0020). **`POST /api/auth/logout`** (UC03, 2026-10-08, ADR-0021): `AuthController.logout` → `LogoutService.logout` (one `@Transactional`: `AccountRepository.clearLastSeen` — waits for the row lock, no `SKIP LOCKED` — **then** `SessionService.revoke`; lock order `accounts → sessions`), 204 without body, body ignored, any role, exempt from BR-TK-17, no audit; `touchLastSeen` now also requires the request's session to be unrevoked (`EXISTS`), so an in-flight request with the same token cannot mark a logged-out staff member online. **`POST /api/me/password`** (UC05, BR-TK-14/17, 2026-10-08, ADR-0022): `MeController` → `ChangePasswordService` (**not** `@Transactional`: reads `(password_hash, locked_until)` readOnly, temporarily locked → 400 BR-TK-09 without BCrypt, BCrypt `matches` + `NewPasswordHasher` (`PasswordPolicy` BR-TK-03 → not same as current → `encode`) while holding no connection) → `ChangePasswordAttemptService.apply` (`@Transactional(noRollbackFor = CurrentPasswordMismatchException.class)`: `findByIdForUpdate`, guard BR-TK-11/09, wrong → `Account.recordFailedLogin` — **the login counter, shared** — + on lock `LOGIN_LOCKED_WARNING` + audit `ACCOUNT_TEMPORARILY_LOCKED`, 400 BR-TK-14; right → `Account.changePassword` (hash, `must_change_password = false`, counter reset) then `SessionService.revokeOthers`); 204, no email, no audit on success. `PasswordPolicy` (BR-TK-03) is shared with registration. **`POST /api/auth/password/forgot` + `/reset`** (UC04, BR-TK-03…07/10/12/13, 2026-10-08, ADR-0023): `AuthController` → `PasswordResetService` (**not** `@Transactional`; every BCrypt runs here with no connection or row lock — public endpoint, BCrypt under the lock would let ~10 parallel requests to one email drain the pool) → `PasswordResetAttemptService`. Forgot: `OtpService.prepare()` (code + BCrypt, every branch) → `issueResetOtp` (`findByEmailForUpdate`, only `ACTIVE` and not `is_locked`, temporarily locked still OK → `OtpService.issuePrepared` → outbox `OTP_PASSWORD_RESET`) → **always 202 with the same body** (`ApiResponse.accepted`, MapStruct `PasswordResetMapper`); BR-TK-07 and `PessimisticLockingFailureException` are caught in the facade **after** rollback (catching inside → `UnexpectedRollbackException` 500; letting them out → 400/409 reveals the email). Reset: `PasswordPolicy` → readOnly `findResetCandidate` → exactly 2 `matches` outside tx (`BcryptDecoy` when no candidate) → `applyReset` (`@Transactional(noRollbackFor = OtpRejectedException.class)`: `findByIdForUpdate` → `OtpService.settleCheckedOtp` (newer code than the one read or expired → BR-TK-05 not counted; wrong → counter committed) → same-as-current BR-TK-03 only after a correct code (rollback keeps the code usable) → `Account.resetPassword` (also clears `locked_until`, `must_change_password`, `last_seen_at`) → `revokeAll` → outbox `PASSWORD_CHANGED`); unknown / ineligible / codeless email → the same 400 BR-TK-05; 204, no audit. `GET /api/me`, `PATCH /api/me/staff-profile`, `GET /api/public/vets` (task 08 parts 1–2) and `/api/me/link-*` (part 3) exist; staff management: create / list / get / resend initial password (T14 part 1), change role / transfer (T14 part 2; transfer 500 until D016), disable / reactivate (T14 part 3; disabling A05–A08 500 until D016), email change (T14 part 4) and account search / lock / unlock (T14 part 5; locking A05–A08 or customers 500 until D016 / D010) exist. **`catalog`** (2026-10-08, UC28–UC31): the 18 staff endpoints of catalog-v1 (`/api/product-categories`, `/products`, `/services`, `/vaccine-types`, `/vaccination-protocols`; read = 5 staff roles, write = SUPER_MANAGER only) with BR-SP-01/02/04/05/06/07 and `CatalogQueryService implements CatalogQueryApi`; the 4 public endpoints (#19–22, UC15/16) are not built. `StockQueryApiPlaceholder` (inventory) and `VaccinationQueryApiPlaceholder` (visit) throw, so turning off `tracksExpiry` (BR-SP-05) and deleting a vaccine type (BR-SP-07) return 500 on the real app until those modules ship; ITs mock both interfaces (`CatalogAdminIT`). Team split: BE-1 = identity, appointment, visit, care; BE-2 = branch, customer, catalog, sales, inventory, boarding, content, report; dates in `docs/timeline_petcare_v15.xlsx`. `docs/architecture/system-overview.md` is the detailed, file-by-file snapshot of what exists — update it when adding a module or infrastructure.
=======
**State as of 2026-10-15 (`manh` = `origin/manh` @ `37fb4de`, merged into `origin/dev` @ `7f19bad` by PR #13. T15 parts 1–4 and Swagger were rebased/committed as `7074c15` (part 1), `388bde7` (part 2), `1434859` (part 3), `55f44cd` (part 4), `37fb4de` (Swagger); the hashes `04bb155` / `5bf7961` / `38ed27e` / `26158e6` quoted below are pre-rebase, as are `90dfc6c` / `b55b906` / `c103a3b` / `0f27381`). **Branch note (2026-10-18):** the T17–T19 paragraph below comes from BE-2's branch `dung` (not pushed) and was merged into this file with the T16 text of `manh`; both branches numbered ADR-0041…0044 and Flyway V13 independently — see `docs/adr/INDEX.md` and Gotchas (duplicate V13 must be renumbered before the branches merge). **T17 customer & pets (2026-10-16, branch `dung` @ `7f19bad` + 6 local commits, not pushed, ADR-0041, BE-2 work):** customer-v1 #8–21 — `GET/POST /api/customers`, `GET/PATCH /api/customers/{id}`, `POST /api/customers/{id}/pets` (RECEPTIONIST writes; RECEPTIONIST / VET / CARETAKER read; ADMIN / SUPER_MANAGER / BRANCH_MANAGER have **no** access), `GET/POST /api/me/pets`, `GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/pets/{id}`, `POST …/mark-deceased`, `POST …/transfer-owner`, `GET/POST …/weights`. `CustomerApi` / `CustomerQueryApi` / `PetApi` / `PetQueryApi` are real (one bean per interface: `CustomerApiService`, `CustomerQueryService`, `PetWeightApiService`, `PetQueryService`; the 3 customer placeholders are deleted → D001, D010 paid, customer register / login / `GET /api/me` / `/api/me/link-*` work). Lock order `accounts → customers (id ↑) → pets → weight_records`; every pet write locks the pet row first (`findByIdForUpdate`), then asks visit / boarding / sales; a module that inserts a row pointing at a pet (visit check-in, appointment, boarding) **must** call `PetQueryApi.findPetForShare` first (`FOR SHARE`, ADR-0041 §3 — `PetLifecycleIT` races + mutation prove it). A customer sees only own pets, anything else (incl. a former owner) is 404 with the same message as a missing id; a pending link decision (BR-TK-19) gives 400 on every pet operation of the customer (contract fixed in `m_customer.py`). Search (#8) is `NamedParameterJdbcTemplate` that appends only the sent conditions, email exact lower-case, name `ILIKE` accent-sensitive; `maskedEmail` / candidate names are masked by `customer/service/Maskers`. Dead pet: second mark = 409, edit / delete / transfer / weight = 400 BR-KH-05. Transfer writes audit `PET_OWNER_TRANSFERRED` and publishes events in the same transaction (no listener exists yet). `createOnlineProfile` has no email parameter → online profile `customers.email` is null. Cross-module reads were implemented **partially in the existing `*Placeholder` classes** of visit / appointment / boarding / sales (other methods still throw) and `content/service/FeedbackQueryService` is new; owners replace them. `V13__pet_customer_fk_indexes.sql` (6 FK indexes, D013 paid; number not yet confirmed with BE-1). `OpenApiContractParityIT` now counts 59 endpoints. `mvn clean verify` 2026-10-16: 3188 unit + 1035 IT green (before T17: 3092 + 942); jar + Mailpit smoke 37/37 (customer register → verify → login → UC07 link by OTP → pets / weights / transfer / deceased / audit). Debts are in the local `docs/dept` (gitignored). **T18 inventory (2026-10-16, branch `dung`, 7 local commits after `6028bd7`, not pushed, ADR-0042 + 0043, BE-2 work):** inventory-v1 #1–17 — `/api/suppliers` (BRANCH_MANAGER, chain-wide), `/api/stock-receipts` (+ `confirm`, `cancel`; FSM 03 #9; BRANCH_MANAGER of own branch, other branch 403), `/api/inventory` (+ `lots`, `min-quantity`; view by A05–A08, lots A05/A06, write A05), `/api/stock-adjustments`. `StockApi` / `StockQueryApi` are real (`StockIssueService`, `StockQueryService`; `StockQueryApiPlaceholder` deleted → turning off `tracksExpiry` is 400 BR-SP-05, no longer 500). **Every change of `stock_lots.quantity` goes through `StockLedger`** (never negative / overflow, always one `stock_movements` row) and every flow locks all lots it touches **in one statement ordered by lot id** (`StockLotDao.lockLots*`, `lockIssuableLots`) — no `LIMIT 1 … FOR UPDATE`; expired = `expiry_date < today` (Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh), FEFO never issues expired lots; `issueForSale` returns `List<Shortage>` and deducts nothing when anything is short. Sales / visit must lock their own document first and then call `StockApi` (none does yet). Receipt codes `PN-yyMM-nnnnn`, adjustments `DC-…` come from `platform/sequence/DocumentCodeGenerator` (table `document_counters`, V14, gap-free, ADR-0043) — reuse it for other documents. Confirming a receipt reads products via `CatalogQueryApi.findProductForShare`; `ProductService.updateProduct` now locks the row first. Inventory list JOINs `products` read-only (deliberate boundary exception, ADR-0042 §6). Audit: `STOCK_RECEIPT_CANCELLED` (confirmed receipts only), `STOCK_ADJUSTED`. `OpenApiContractParityIT` counts 76 endpoints. `StockReportApi` / ST08 left for T44. `mvn clean verify` 2026-10-16: 3232 unit + 1117 IT green (before: 3188 + 1035); jar + docker smoke 27/27. Debts in the local `docs/dept/T18-kho.md` (gitignored). **T19 Order core (2026-10-17, branch `dung`, 5 local commits after `ec9c6fd`, not pushed, ADR-0044, BE-2 work):** sales-v1 #3–6 — `GET /api/orders/{orderId}` (A05–A08 of the order's branch), `POST /api/orders/{orderId}/lines` (RECEPTIONIST → GOODS, VET → SERVICE group MEDICAL, CARETAKER → SERVICE group GROOMING; VET / CARETAKER must be the visit's `assignee_id`; OPEN takes every line, PENDING only GOODS), `PATCH` / `DELETE …/lines/{lineId}`. `SalesOrder` (table `orders`, not `Order`: JPQL keyword) + `OrderLine`; `totalAmount` is **recomputed from the lines** (`addExact`; overflow = 400 `VALIDATION_FAILED`); price + name snapshotted when the line is added (BR-BH-03, product read `FOR SHARE`). FSM `OrderTransitionHandler` has the 10 transitions of 03 #5 — source-specific conditions are service guards (`cancelByVisit` requires OPEN). **Every write locks the `orders` row first** (`findByIdForUpdate` / `findByVisitIdForUpdate`; a line is found by an unlocked read of its order id, then locked, then re-read); the REST layer never locks `visits` (reads `VisitQueryApi.findVisit` unlocked), so the order is `visits → orders → products → stock lots`. Line permissions live in the pure `OrderLinePolicy` and feed both the guards and the `canEdit` / `canDelete` flags: VACCINE / BOARDING never edited directly (400 BR-BH-02), DRUG only through the prescription, SERVICE only by `owner_account_id` and locked when PENDING (403), GOODS by any receptionist; 403 carries `BR-BH-02` via `OrderLineAccessDeniedException`. PATCH only lowers quantity; `serviceId` only on the auto-generated SERVICE line of a VISIT order (same group as the role, price re-snapshotted). Order codes `DH-yyMM-nnnnn` from `DocumentCodeGenerator`. `VisitOrderApi` (10 methods, `MANDATORY`) and `OrderQueryApi` are real (`OrderQueryApiPlaceholder` deleted); `VisitQueryApiPlaceholder.findVisit` reads `visits` with SQL. **Obligation for visit (BE-1):** record an injection = `addVaccineLine` → `lockFefoLot` → INSERT `vaccinations` → `issueForVaccination`; delete one = delete `vaccinations` → `removeVaccineLine` → `revertVaccination` (06 §3 corrected). Not built: sales-v1 #1 #2 #7 #8 #10 (T29), payments / shifts #11–21 (T30), `abort-checkout` + `BoardingOrderApi` (T43). `V15__order_lines_index.sql`. `OpenApiContractParityIT` counts 80 endpoints. `mvn clean verify` 2026-10-17: 3899 unit + 1165 IT green (before: 3232 + 1117); jar + docker smoke 35/35 (xem, thêm / sửa / xóa dòng theo từng vai trò, PENDING / CANCELLED, Swagger). Debts in the local `docs/dept/T19-order.md` (gitignored). **Rebase repair (uncommitted, 2026-10-19):** `aa051cb` (= `origin/manh`) did not compile — the rebase onto `dung` had committed conflict markers in `customer/api/PetQueryApi.java`, `OpenApiContractParityIT`, `SchemaMigrationIT`, re-added `CustomerQueryApiPlaceholder` / `PetQueryApiPlaceholder` (BE-2 had deleted them) and left `AppointmentQueryApiPlaceholder` beside the real beans (two beans per interface), and BE-2's real `CustomerQueryService` / `PetQueryService` lacked the T16 batch methods. Fixed: markers resolved (both sides), the 3 placeholders + their unit tests deleted, `findContacts` (JPQL constructor `CustomerRepository.findContactsByIds`) and `findPets` (`PetRepository.findRowsByIds` + `WeightRecordRepository.findCurrentWeights`, `DISTINCT ON`) added — one SQL per call, no entity loaded, empty → no SQL; `V13__appointment_code_sequence.sql` renamed **V16** (V13–V15 are BE-2's, pushed on `origin/dung`; a dev DB that ran the old manh V13 needs `docker compose down -v` or `flyway repair`); `OpenApiContractParityIT` = 6 contract files, 86 endpoints. **T25 part 1 (uncommitted, 2026-10-19, ADR-0047 — Lịch hẹn#5 `cancelByClinic`, D034 paid):** `appointment/listener/{BranchClinicCancellationListener, PetDeceasedListener, PetOwnerTransferredListener}` (first `@EventListener`s in `src/main`; one-line delegation, no `@Async` / `@Transactional`) → `appointment/service/AppointmentClinicCancellationService` (`MANDATORY`, readOnly guard, no `noRollbackFor`, no catch): ids deduped + ascending → `AppointmentRepository.findAllByIdInForUpdate` (≤ 100 ids per statement, `ORDER BY id`, `FOR NO KEY UPDATE`; row count ≠ ids → `IllegalStateException`) → `now` truncated to µs **after** the locks → status re-checked under the lock (not `BOOKED` → skipped, no error) → `Appointment.cancel(CLINIC, ClinicCancelCause.reason(), now, false)` → `saveAndFlush` → `ClinicCancellationNotices`. Pet events use `findBookedIdsByPetId` (ids only) and cancel **every** `BOOKED` of the pet, also past slots ST05 has not marked; transfer notifies `appointments.customer_id` (old owner). No pet lock (customer must take advisory 1001 before `pets` — D027 still open), no branch lock, no reschedule-count change, no `BookingRestrictionApi`, no audit, no `BranchScope`. Notifications `APPOINTMENT_CANCELLED_BY_CLINIC` (EMAIL) + `_APP` (IN_APP, link `/appointments`), seeded by **V17**; recipients per BR-TB-02: verified account → IN_APP + EMAIL to the **account** email via new `identity/api/AccountContactApi.findContacts` (`AccountContactService`, readOnly joining the caller's tx, JPQL constructor with a `pending` flag), `PENDING` account = no account, counter profile with email → EMAIL, nothing → INFO `APPOINTMENT_CLINIC_CANCEL_NO_CHANNEL`; names batched ≤ 100 per module, missing / duplicate id → `IllegalStateException`; every required variable checked non-null in the tx because `NotificationApi.enqueue` does not check `required_vars` (only ST20 does, after commit → silent `FAILED`). A listener failure rolls back the whole publisher use case (holiday / opening hours / `deceased_on` / new owner + its audit). Tests: `AppointmentClinicCancellationServiceTest`, `ClinicCancellationNoticesTest`, `ClinicCancellationListenersTest`, `AccountContactServiceTest`, `module/appointment/ClinicCancellationIT` (HTTP of the four publishers, real listeners + ST20 workers + GreenMail, `@MockitoBean BoardingQueryApi`: columns + GET #4, recipients, payload ⊆/⊇ template vars, trigger on the 2nd of 3 rows rolls everything back, races with customer cancel / check-in / two events on one row, 150 vs 1 appointment Hibernate query count), `BranchAdminIT` now mocks `AppointmentClinicCancellationService` (its event ids are fake). On the real app adding a holiday / narrowing hours of an `ACTIVE` branch still 500s at `BoardingQueryApi` before the event (D033). **T16 part 5 (uncommitted, 2026-10-16, ADR-0046 — `AppointmentApi` + `AppointmentQueryApi` real, no endpoint, no migration):** `appointment/service/AppointmentVisitService implements AppointmentApi` — `checkIn(appointmentId, branchId, Instant at)` → `CheckInInfo(appointmentId, customerId, petId, serviceId, serviceGroup, scheduledAt)`, `completeByVisit(appointmentId)`, `cancelByVisit(appointmentId, Instant at)` (`actorId` dropped; no column stores it). Every method `@Transactional(MANDATORY)`, no `noRollbackFor`, no `try/catch`, readOnly guard like `AppointmentLocks`; locks **only** the `appointments` row (`findByIdForUpdate`, last step of `advisory(pet) → branches → appointments`), every guard reads status **after** the lock, checks before `saveAndFlush` (a rejection writes nothing, a DB error surfaces inside the call). `checkIn`: 404 → 409 (not `BOOKED`) → 400 BR-TN-02 (other branch / `CheckInWindow` with `visit.check_in_early_minutes` and `appointment.no_show_after_minutes`, both ends inclusive) — deliberate deviation from convention 05, like ADR-0045 Q6; `Appointment.checkIn()` / `complete()`. `cancelByVisit`: explicit `CHECKED_IN` check, `Appointment.cancel(STAFF, null, at µs, false)`, never calls `BookingRestrictionApi`. Missing id in `completeByVisit` / `cancelByVisit` = `IllegalStateException` 500 (FK `visits.appointment_id`). **Obligations for visit (T26):** call inside the write tx of Visit#1/#5/#6, **never catch** these exceptions (rollback-only → `UnexpectedRollbackException`), call after all its `branches` / advisory locks, no re-check of its own guards here. `appointment/service/AppointmentLookupService implements AppointmentQueryApi` — class `@Transactional(readOnly = true)` joins the caller's write tx; JPQL `SELECT new BookedSlot(…)` (no entity loaded, one SQL per call), `findBookedFrom` / `findBookedByCustomer` BOOKED only ordered `slot_date, slot_start, id` (no upper bound, no paging — branch needs the full list for BR-LH-10), `existsByPet` / `existsByCustomer` any status; customer (T17) must call `existsBy…` after the pet advisory lock (D027). `AppointmentQueryApiPlaceholder` + its test deleted. `branch/service/ScheduleImpactFinder.describe` (BE-2's file, fixed by BE-1) now collects pet / customer ids of appointments **and** stays, dedups, batches ≤ 100 into `findPets` / `findContacts` (duplicate id → `IllegalStateException`, empty → no call). Tests: `AppointmentVisitServiceTest`, `AppointmentLookupServiceTest`, `ScheduleImpactFinderTest`, `module/appointment/AppointmentVisitTxIT` (10: commit / rollback with a test `VisitLike` bean inserting real `visits` rows; caller swallowing the error cannot commit; trigger on UPDATE surfaces inside the call), `AppointmentVisitApiIT` (9: check-in ∥ cancel / reschedule / double check-in via row lock + `pg_stat_activity`, quota / BR-LH-05 unchanged after check-in / complete, `cancelByVisit` frees quota and is no violation, `checkInReady` list ⇔ what `checkIn` accepts, boundaries), `AppointmentQueryApiIT` (5, incl. holiday waiting for an in-flight booking → 400 BR-LH-10), `AppointmentQueryApiPlaceholderIT` (2: holiday with a BOOKED appointment still 500 at `BoardingQueryApi`, nothing written), `AppointmentQueryCountIT` (4: Hibernate Statistics — 1 SQL per query with 1 and 50 rows, 2 per write call), `BranchAdminIT` (mocks `findPets` / `findContacts`). Mutations killed: `REQUIRES_NEW`, `save` instead of `saveAndFlush`, `noRollbackFor`, `findById` before the lock, `findBookedFrom` without the status filter, and 7 unit-level ones (cancel from `BOOKED`, `late_cancel = true`, no branch check, window ±1 minute, readOnly guard). New debt **D034** (nobody listens to `BranchClinicCancellationEvent` — must exist before or together with a real `BoardingQueryApi`, D033). On the real app: branch hours / holiday of an `ACTIVE` branch still 500 at `BoardingQueryApi` (D033), customer lock 500 at `CustomerQueryApi` (D010); no real caller of `AppointmentApi` until T26. **T16 part 4 (committed `9a8a7a0`, 2026-10-15, ADR-0045 — reschedule / cancel):** `POST /api/appointments/{id}/reschedule` (appointment-v1 #5) and `/cancel` (#6), `@PreAuthorize` CUSTOMER / RECEPTIONIST → `appointment/service/AppointmentChangeService` (one `@Transactional` each, no `noRollbackFor`, no `try/catch`). Authorization before any lock on the projection `AppointmentRepository.findAccessRow` (customer_id, pet_id, branch_id — all `updatable = false`; projection so `findByIdForUpdate` never returns a stale entity): customer → own `customer_id` (another customer's = same 404), receptionist → `BranchScope.check` 403. Reschedule locks `lockPet → lockForBooking → findByIdForUpdate` (`FOR NO KEY UPDATE`, `MANDATORY` on the repository) → `now` → `validateTransition(status, BOOKED)` 409 **before any 400** (deliberate deviation from convention 05, Q6) → same slot = 200 no write (Q5) → BR-LH-06 (turns `< appointment.max_reschedules`, customer `now + reschedule_min_lead_hours ≤ start` inclusive, receptionist before start) → BR-LH-04 (`BookingWindow`) → BR-LH-02 (`SlotGrid`) → BR-LH-05 via new `existsActiveSameSlotExcept` / `existsActiveSameGroupDayExcept` (excludes itself; BOOKED count not checked) → BR-LH-03 → `Appointment.reschedule` (count +1, **`reminded_at = NULL`** so ST03 reminds the new slot, Q9) → `saveAndFlush`. BR-LH-01, 09, KH-05, TK-19 not re-checked (Q4, Q7, Q8, Q10); no `PetQueryApi` call for rules. Cancel locks only the row → `now = clock.instant().truncatedTo(MICROS)` (POST response must equal GET #4; same instant becomes `starts_at`) → status ≠ BOOKED → **explicit** `InvalidStateTransitionException` 409 (the FSM has CHECKED_IN → CANCELLED #8 for Visit#6 only) → `now ≥ start` 400 BR-LH-07 → `late = (start − now) < appointment.late_cancel_hours` (exactly 12 h not late) → `Appointment.cancel` (`cancel_source` CUSTOMER / STAFF from the role) → `saveAndFlush` → if late `BookingRestrictionApi.evaluateAfterViolation` (**D030 paid**: `MANDATORY`, advisory **1002** per customer via `AppointmentLocks.lockCustomerViolations` — always the last lock; active restriction → nothing; count violations `> max(at − booking_restriction.window_days, starts_at of the latest restriction incl. ended / lifted)` — assumption Q3, each violation feeds one restriction — from `appointments.countViolationsAfter` (late cancel by `cancelled_at`, NO_SHOW by slot time) + `BoardingQueryApi.countViolations` (contract now `> since`); ≥ threshold → `BookingRestriction.start`; must stay READ COMMITTED). Response via new package-private helper `AppointmentResponder` (extracted from `AppointmentQueryService`, built by each service with `new`, so `AppointmentQueryServiceTest` is untouched); messages "Đã đổi khung giờ" / "Đã hủy lịch hẹn"; cancel body optional (`@RequestBody(required = false)`; `""` with JSON content type → null, proven by IT). On the real app: customer 500 (D010), receptionist 500 at the name lookup (D031), every late cancel 500 at `BoardingQueryApi` (new debt **D033**) — always rolled back (`RescheduleCancelPlaceholderIT`). No migration, no audit. Tests: `AppointmentChangeServiceTest`, `BookingRestrictionServiceTest`, `AppointmentLocksTest` (+1002), `RescheduleCancelRequestValidationTest`, `AppointmentControllerSecurityTest`, `module/appointment/RescheduleCancelIT` (54: response ≡ GET #4 ≡ DB, quota occupancy moves, list follows, every rule + 409 precedence, late-cancel boundary, restriction lifecycle, triggers on UPDATE / INSERT roll back, races via `whileHeld` — last slot, double reschedule, cancel ∥ reschedule, double cancel, concurrent third violation → exactly one row, holiday while waiting, unauthorized takes no lock), `RescheduleCancelPlaceholderIT`; `OpenApiContractParityIT` now 51 endpoints. Baseline before: 3276 unit + 1113 IT (`b79fc98`, `mvn clean verify` 2026-10-15). **T16 part 3 (committed `b79fc98`, 2026-10-15, ADR-0044 — view appointments):** `GET /api/appointments` (appointment-v1 #3; `@PreAuthorize` CUSTOMER / BRANCH_MANAGER / RECEPTIONIST) and `GET /api/appointments/{appointmentId}` (#4; + VET, CARETAKER) → `appointment/service/AppointmentQueryService` (both `@Transactional(readOnly = true, isolation = REPEATABLE_READ)`, no lock, no write). Scope: customer always `customer_id` = own profile via `CustomerQueryApi.findCustomerIdByAccountId` (owner **at booking time** — after a pet transfer the old owner keeps the history; never through `BranchScope`); staff `BranchScope.resolve(branchId)` before any DB read (other branch → 403). #4: missing **or another customer's = same 404** "Không tìm thấy lịch hẹn #id"; staff of another branch → 403 via `check` after the lookup. Filters `branchId?, date?, from?, to?, status?, petId?, customerId?, checkInReady?` as a JPA `Specification` that only adds sent conditions (`AppointmentRepository extends JpaSpecificationExecutor`); everything ANDs with the scope; `from > to` → empty page; blank optional param = not sent; repeated `status=` keeps the first value (Spring, pinned by IT). `checkInReady=true` = BOOKED + today (VN) + `slot_start − visit.check_in_early_minutes ≤ now ≤ slot_start + appointment.no_show_after_minutes`, both ends inclusive, pure `appointment/service/CheckInWindow` (lower bound before midnight → 00:00, upper after midnight → no upper bound, never `LocalTime.MAX`) — **obligation: Visit#1 (T26) and ST05 (T25) reuse it**. Order `slot_date, slot_start, id` ascending; page/size clamped 0 / 1–100. Names per page in one batch call per module: **new** `PetQueryApi.findPets(Collection<Long>)` and `CustomerQueryApi.findContacts(Collection<Long>)` (contract change for BE-2, 06 §6; placeholders throw), `CatalogQueryApi.findServices` (does not filter inactive), `BranchQueryApi.findBranch` per distinct branch; joined by id (missing / duplicate id → 500); **empty page calls no module**. Flags: CUSTOMER / RECEPTIONIST via `AppointmentActions.forViewer`, BRANCH_MANAGER / VET / CARETAKER → `AppointmentActions.Flags.NONE`. Contract #3 notes / 400 / 403 rewritten, **#4 gained 400** (malformed id, BR-TK-17). Real error order: 401 → 400 BR-TK-17 (interceptor `preHandle`) → 400 `MALFORMED_REQUEST` → 403 role → 403 scope / 404. On the real app: customer 500 (D010), staff 500 once the page has rows (D031), empty page 200 (`AppointmentViewPlaceholderIT`). New debt **D032** (no `(branch_id, slot_date)` index for the all-status branch-day list; exact count + OFFSET). Tests: `CheckInWindowTest`, `AppointmentQueryServiceTest`, `AppointmentActionsTest` (+NONE), `AppointmentControllerSecurityTest` (per-handler role map), `module/appointment/AppointmentViewIT` (41: shape vs SQL, name join by id + one batch call per page, transfer, inactive service, scope, filters, sort, paging, checkInReady boundaries + [CFG], param errors, precedence, 401/405, no write via `xmin`, EXPLAIN of the hot shapes on 20 000 rows), `AppointmentViewPlaceholderIT`; `OpenApiContractParityIT` now 49 endpoints; `BookAppointmentIT` 405 case moved from GET to PUT. Mutations (drop `customer_id` scope, drop `resolve`, drop the upper bound, `≤`→`<` at either end, drop `REPEATABLE_READ`) each turn a test red. Baseline before: 3239 unit + 1069 IT (HEAD `e5fc585`, counted from reports, 2026-10-15). **T16 part 2 (committed `e5fc585`, 2026-10-15, ADR-0043 — first appointment write):** `POST /api/appointments` (appointment-v1 #2, UC39), `@PreAuthorize` CUSTOMER (channel `ONLINE`) / RECEPTIONIST (`COUNTER`, `BranchScope.check` before any lock) → `appointment/service/AppointmentBookingService.bookAppointment` (one `@Transactional`, no `noRollbackFor`, ADR-0041 §4 skeleton). Only authorization runs before the locks: customer → `CustomerQueryApi.findCustomerIdByAccountId` (none → 500) → `lockPet` → `PetQueryApi.findPet` **once, right after the pet lock** (missing **or another customer's pet = same 404** "Không tìm thấy thú cưng #id"; supersedes the ownership part of ADR-0041 §1 because BE-2's implementation may return a cached entity on a second read) → `catalog.findService` 404 → `lockForBooking` (missing / customer + DRAFT → 404) → `now = clock.instant()` **after** the locks → BR-LH-01 (receptionist + DRAFT, service inactive / not MEDICAL·GROOMING / not enabled) → BR-TK-19, BR-LH-09 (customer only) → BR-KH-05 → BR-LH-04 (`BookingWindow`) → BR-LH-02 (`SlotGrid.isSlotStart`) → BR-LH-05 (a) `countBookedByPet ≥ appointment.max_booked_per_pet`, (b) same slot, (c) same group same day — (b)/(c) count BOOKED + CHECKED_IN + COMPLETED on every branch → BR-LH-03 (`countActiveInSlot ≥ quotaFor`) → `validateInitial(BOOKED)` → `nextCode` → `Appointment.book` → `saveAndFlush` → `201 "Đã đặt lịch hẹn"`. Response `AppointmentResponse` (24 contract keys, MapStruct `AppointmentMapper`, `visitId` ignored → null, D028), `canReschedule` / `canCancel` from pure `AppointmentActions` (BOOKED; cancel before slot start; reschedule also `< max_reschedules` and, for customers, ≥ `reschedule_min_lead_hours`, boundary allowed). `note` stripped, blank → null; 3 ids `@Positive` (`lockPet` rejects ≤ 0 with 500). `BookingRestrictionService implements BookingRestrictionApi`: `isRestricted` real (`starts_at ≤ at < ends_at AND lifted_at IS NULL`, entity `BookingRestriction`), `evaluateAfterViolation` throws (D030). No audit, no notification, `FollowUpApi` not called (Care Task#6, D029). Contract #2 changed: 403 only "Lễ tân đặt lịch cho chi nhánh khác", 404 includes another customer's pet. On the real app both roles get 500 (customer: `CustomerQueryApi` D010; receptionist: `PetQueryApi` D031) with nothing written and no lock left (`BookAppointmentPlaceholderIT`). Tests: `AppointmentBookingServiceTest`, `AppointmentActionsTest`, `AppointmentMapperTest`, `BookAppointmentRequestValidationTest`, `BookingRestrictionServiceTest`, `AppointmentControllerSecurityTest`, `module/appointment/BookAppointmentIT` (SQL stand-in `JdbcPetCustomerModule` for both BE-2 interfaces; `whileHeld` holds `branches FOR UPDATE` or advisory 1001 in another tx and waits on `pg_stat_activity`: last slot, 2-BOOKED / same-day across branches, holiday / quota 0 / service off committed while waiting, `now` after the lock, pet deceased while waiting, double submit, distinct codes; index plans on 20 000 rows inserted in a rolled-back tx + `ANALYZE` — with an empty table the planner picks any index), `BookAppointmentPlaceholderIT`. Malformed `slotStart` (`25:00`, `8:30`, `08:30:00`, `830`) is 400 `MALFORMED_REQUEST` (proven by IT). **T16 part 1 (committed `4d98452`, 2026-10-15, ADR-0042 — first appointment endpoint):** `GET /api/branches/{branchId}/available-slots?serviceId=&date=` (appointment-v1 #1, UC39), `@PreAuthorize` CUSTOMER / RECEPTIONIST only (`appointment/controller/AvailableSlotController`) → `appointment/service/AvailableSlotService.listAvailableSlots` (`@Transactional(readOnly = true, isolation = REPEATABLE_READ)`, one snapshot, **no lock**, no write). Order: 401 → 400 BR-TK-17 → 400 `MALFORMED_REQUEST` (before 403) → 403 role → receptionist `BranchScope.check` 403 before any DB read (customers never go through `BranchScope`, ADR-0006 §4; `switch` on exactly two roles, unknown role → 500) → branch missing 404 (**customer + DRAFT = same 404**) → service missing 404 → receptionist + DRAFT 400 "Chi nhánh chưa nhận đặt lịch (BR-LH-01)" → service inactive / BOARDING / disabled at branch 400 "Dịch vụ không đặt được tại chi nhánh này (BR-LH-01)". BR-TK-19 / BR-LH-09 not checked here (booking #2). Window `appointment/service/BookingWindow` (reuse it in parts 2/4): customer `date ≤ today(VN) + appointment.max_advance_days` (**by date**) and `slotStart ≥ now + appointment.min_lead_hours` (**by instant**, inclusive); receptionist `slotStart > now`; outside / holiday / closed → `200 []`. `SlotGrid.slotStarts(openingRangesOn)` → `AppointmentRepository.countActiveBySlot` (native `GROUP BY slot_start`, statuses as literals for partial index `ix_appointments_quota`, time returned as seconds of day) + `BranchQueryApi.quotaFor` per slot; `SlotAvailability.full()` = `booked ≥ quota`; `SlotResponse` via MapStruct `SlotMapper` (`unmappedTargetPolicy = ERROR`, `HH:mm` by `@JsonFormat`). `OpenApiConfig` tag `Slots`; `OpenApiContractParityIT.DOCUMENTED` now includes `appointment-v1.yaml`. No migration, no debt. Tests: `BookingWindowTest`, `SlotMapperTest`, `AvailableSlotServiceTest`, `module/appointment/AvailableSlotsIT` (36; `AppointmentItSupport` gained opening hours / holiday / branch service / quota / staff / customer-account helpers and deletes branch children + sessions + staff_profiles before services). Note: Spring Boot already binds `LocalDate` query params as ISO — `@DateTimeFormat(iso = DATE)` is kept for explicitness only (mutation removing it stays green). **T16 part 0 (committed `4d98452`, 2026-10-15, ADR-0041 — appointment foundation, no endpoint, no call site in running code yet):** `appointment/entity/Appointment` (all 19 V1 columns; `reschedule_count` SMALLINT → `short`; FKs as `Long`; `serviceGroup` = `catalog.api.ServiceGroup` snapshot; no `visit_id` column — debt D028) + enums `AppointmentStatus`, `AppointmentChannel`, `AppointmentCancelSource`; `appointment/fsm/AppointmentTransitionHandler` (03 §3 exactly, `fsmName()` = "lịch hẹn"); approved command names `bookAppointment` #1, `rescheduleAppointment` #2, `checkIn` #3, `cancelAppointment` #4, `cancelByClinic` #5, `markNoShow` #6, `completeByVisit` #7, `cancelByVisit` #8. **Lock order for every appointment write: `advisory(pet) → branches → appointments`, authorization done before any lock.** `appointment/service/AppointmentLocks.lockPet` = `pg_advisory_xact_lock(1001, pet_id % 2147483647)` (transaction-scoped, BR-LH-05 across branches; namespace registry in ADR-0041); `branch/api/BranchApi.lockForBooking(branchId)` (BE-1 in BE-2's module: native scalar `SELECT status … FOR NO KEY UPDATE` via `BranchRepository.lockStatusForBooking`, same row every branch schedule / quota / service write locks, so booking ∥ holiday / opening hours / quota / service toggle and two bookings for the last slot serialise;
