New debt D026 (branch, catalog). **T15 part 5 (2026-10-14, docs only):** notification settings (care-v1 #9–10, UC88) split off from T15 — PO has not decided the content (care-v1 Q1) and no consumer (ST03 T25, ST04 T39) exists yet → debt **D025**, built in the same PR as ST03 (at the latest ST04); `accounts.notification_settings` stays `'{}'`, every reminder follows BR-TB-02. **T15 part 4 (committed `26158e6`, 2026-10-14, ADR-0039):** in-app notifications of the signed-in user, care-v1 #5 `GET /api/me/notifications` (`unreadOnly?`, `page`, `size`), #6 `GET …/unread-count`, #7 `POST …/{notificationId}/read`, #8 `POST …/read-all` (UC88 read part; #9–10 settings not built — care-v1 Q1 TBD). `care/controller/MyNotificationController` (`@PreAuthorize("isAuthenticated()")` on every handler, **not** exempt from BR-TK-17) → `care/service/MyNotificationService` (owner always `BranchScope.current().accountId()`, no body, no account parameter; reads `readOnly` at default READ COMMITTED — count and page may differ by one row while ST20 delivers, unlike ADR-0038; writes plain `@Transactional`, no audit / outbox / event). `NotificationRepository` native queries with constant conditions (two read methods split by `unreadOnly`, never `:x IS NULL OR`), `ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC`, page/size clamped like #28. #7: one `UPDATE … SET read_at = :now, updated_at = :now WHERE id = :id AND account_id = :accountId AND read_at IS NULL`; only when 0 rows → `existsByIdAndAccountId` → already read 204 (keeps the first `readAt`), missing **or someone else's** → 404 "Không tìm thấy thông báo #<id>". #8: same UPDATE by `account_id`; rows ST20 commits after the statement snapshot stay unread. Bulk UPDATEs set `updated_at` themselves (ADR-0015). Response `NotificationResponse` (7 contract keys, MapStruct `NotificationMapper`, no `accountId` / `updatedAt`), `UnreadCountResponse(int count)`. Index V1 `ix_notifications_account_read_created`: counts and UPDATEs use it fully; both page queries still Sort the account's rows (EXPLAIN 2026-10-14) — debt **D022**; no cleanup of `notifications` — **D023**; any authenticated staff request (incl. polling) refreshes `last_seen_at`, so a polling tab keeps staff "online" (BR-TN-06) — **D024**. No migration. Tests: `MyNotificationServiceTest`, `NotificationMapperTest`, `MyNotificationControllerSecurityTest`, `module/care/MyNotificationIT` (25 cases; own accounts per test — `InAppNotificationIT` deletes the whole table; row-lock wait proves #7/#8 keep a concurrent first `readAt`; EXPLAIN in a rolled-back transaction). **T15 part 3 (committed `38ed27e`, 2026-10-14, ADR-0038):** `GET /api/audit-logs` (#36, UC11), `@PreAuthorize` ADMIN only (`AuditLogController`, GET only — other methods 405; 403 not audited; viewing is **not** audited, Q5). `AuditLogQueryService.listAuditLogs` `@Transactional(readOnly = true, isolation = REPEATABLE_READ)` — first non-default isolation in the repo, so count and page share one snapshot on an insert-heavy table (`BEGIN READ ONLY`; Spring/Hikari reset the connection, ADR-0020 L22). Read path `identity/repository/AuditLogQueryRepository` = `NamedParameterJdbcTemplate` + builder `AuditLogQuery` that only appends conditions actually sent (**never** `:x IS NULL OR` — generic plans lose the index), 11 columns, `ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT/OFFSET` (deliberate deviation from convention 02). Filters exact match, closed `[from, to]` (ISO with offset, `+` must be `%2B`), blank `action`/`entityType` = not sent, unknown value → empty page (never 404), page/size clamped like #28. Order: wrong type → 400 `MALFORMED_REQUEST`, `action`/`entityType` > 50 → 400 `VALIDATION_FAILED` (both before `@PreAuthorize`) → 403 → R1 `from > to` (on `to`), R2 `entityId` without `entityType` (on `entityType`), R3 `action` without `actorAccountId` or `entityType + entityId` needs `from` + `to` ≤ 31 days (on `from`, `AuditLogFilter.MAX_ACTION_WINDOW`, not [CFG]) → 400 `VALIDATION_FAILED` via class-level `@ValidAuditLogFilter` + `Validator` in the service, fixed R1→R2→R3 order (private `ConstraintViolationException` subclass: the stock constructor copies into a `HashSet`); not BR codes. Index map proven by EXPLAIN: actor → `ix_audit_logs_actor_created_at`, entity → `ix_audit_logs_entity`, range / none / action+range → `ix_audit_logs_created_at`. Response `AuditLogEntryResponse` (11 keys, no join: `actorEmail` as recorded, `actorAccountId` kept for deleted accounts; `beforeData`/`afterData` raw jsonb via `@JsonRawValue`; `createdAt` `Instant`). `AuditRecorder.snapshot` now rejects snapshots that are not a JSON object (List / String / number → `IllegalArgumentException`, both `record` and `recordIndependently`). New debts **D020** (exact count + OFFSET), **D021** (no index on `action`). No migration. Tests: `AuditLogFilterValidationTest`, `AuditLogQueryTest`, `AuditLogQueryServiceTest`, `AuditLogMapperTest`, `AuditLogControllerSecurityTest`, `AuditRecorderTest` (+2), `module/identity/AuditLogViewIT` (31 cases; audit rows in year 2003, one 2-day window and one `actor_account_id` range per test; EXPLAIN on 60 000 rows inserted in a rolled-back transaction; mutations dropping the isolation or `readOnly` turn it red). Baseline before the change: 3037 unit + 875 IT (`5bf7961`, run 2026-10-13); after it (`mvn clean verify` 2026-10-14): 3067 unit + 906 IT green, docker-compose smoke (ADMIN + SUPER_MANAGER by SQL → login → own `LOGIN_SUCCEEDED` row → PUT [CFG] → `SYSTEM_CONFIG_UPDATED` with object before/after → `%2B` range → R1/R2/R3/malformed 400 → 403/401/405 → GET writes no audit → size clamp) 25/25. **T15 part 2 (committed `5bf7961`, 2026-10-13, ADR-0037):** `GET /api/notification-templates` (#33), `PUT /api/notification-templates/{code}` (#34), `POST …/{code}/restore-default` (#35), `@PreAuthorize` ADMIN only (`NotificationTemplateController`, 403 not audited). No facade: `NotificationTemplateAdminService` (one `@Transactional` per write, no `noRollbackFor`, catches nothing; list readOnly sorted by `code`). Order #34: Bean Validation (`UpdateTemplateRequest`: `subject` `@NotBlank @Size(max = 255)` for **every** channel — contract changed `subject?` → `subject`; `body` `@NotBlank`, no length limit → D018) → `NotificationTemplateRepository.findByCodeForUpdate` (`FOR NO KEY UPDATE`, before any other read of the row) → 404 "Không tìm thấy mẫu thông báo #<code>" → `TemplateVariables.scan` (package-private, strict grammar: braces only as `{name}` with name ∈ `allowed_vars`, case-sensitive; unknown token, stray `{`/`}`, missing `required_vars` over subject ∪ body) → 400 BR-QT-14 naming **every** bad variable, one message → content identical (verbatim, no strip / CRLF normalisation) → no UPDATE but **still audited** before = after (BR-QT-15, unlike ADR-0036) → `NotificationTemplate.changeContent` → `flush()` → audit `NOTIFICATION_TEMPLATE_UPDATED` (`entity = notification_templates`, `entityId` null, before/after `{code, subject, body}`). #35 same with `restoreDefault`, audit `NOTIFICATION_TEMPLATE_RESTORED`, defaults not re-scanned (IT proves every seeded default passes). Response `NotificationTemplateResponse` (7 contract keys, `channel` = `NotificationApi.Channel`, `@JsonProperty("isDefault")`, `isDefault` = character-exact equality with `default_*`). The ST20 renderer (`care/service/NotificationTemplateRenderer`) is untouched; scanner ↔ renderer kept in sync by `NotificationTemplateSyntaxParityTest`; renderer substitutes sequentially → new debt D019; no newline ban in subject (Jakarta Mail folds lines — `NotificationDispatchServiceTest.lineBreakInSubjectCannotInjectHeaders`). Edits apply from the next read after commit, including outbox rows queued before. No migration. Tests: `TemplateVariablesTest`, `NotificationTemplateSyntaxParityTest`, `NotificationTemplateAdminServiceTest`, `NotificationTemplateTest`, `NotificationTemplateAdminMapperTest`, `UpdateTemplateRequestValidationTest`, `module/identity/NotificationTemplateAdminIT` (32 cases; `@AfterEach` restores every template to its defaults). Baseline before the change (`04bb155`, `mvn clean verify` 2026-10-13): 2970 unit + 843 IT green; after it: 3037 unit + 875 IT green, docker-compose smoke (ADMIN by SQL → #33 → 403 for SUPER_MANAGER → BR-QT-14 / 400 / 404 → valid PUT → forgot password → Mailpit shows the edited subject and body → #35 twice → audits UPDATED + 2 × RESTORED, the second before = after) 19/19. **T15 part 1 (committed `04bb155`, 2026-10-13, ADR-0036):** `GET /api/system-configs` (#31) and `PUT /api/system-configs/{key}` (#32), `@PreAuthorize` ADMIN only (`SystemConfigController`, 403 not audited). Facade `SystemConfigAdminService` (**not** `@Transactional`) → `SystemConfigEditService.updateConfig` (one `@Transactional`, no `noRollbackFor`, catches nothing) → then `SystemConfigService.reload()` (now `synchronized`; replaces ADR-0004's `afterCommit` idea — DB reads in `afterCommit` still join the committed tx). Order: key not in `ConfigKey` → 404 "Không tìm thấy tham số #<key>" before any DB access → `SystemConfigRepository.findByKeyForUpdate` (`FOR NO KEY UPDATE`) for the key **and its cross-key partner, ascending key, before any other read of those rows** (stale persistence-context trap, ADR-0029 §4) → `ConfigValues.parseRequest` (shared with `load`; TIME `HH:mm` only, DECIMAL no exponent) + locked row's min–max + `ConfigConstraints` (`visit.late_priority_minutes < appointment.no_show_after_minutes`, `boarding.min_nights ≤ boarding.max_nights`; also checked by `load` at startup) → 400 BR-QT-13 whose message shows the **effective** allowed range (wrong type is BR-QT-13 too) → same canonical value → 200, no write, no audit (still reloads) → `SystemConfig.changeValue(canonical, actor)` → `flush()` → audit `SYSTEM_CONFIG_UPDATED` (`entity = system_configs`, `entityId` null, before/after `{key, value}`). Reload failing after commit → log `SYSTEM_CONFIG_RELOAD_FAILED`, 500; retrying the same value heals the cache. Live-read keys (`otp.max_failed_attempts`, `login.*`…) apply to work in progress by design (ADR-0036 §9). New debt **D018** (no request body size limit, app-wide). No migration. Tests: `ConfigValuesTest`, `ConfigConstraintsTest`, `SystemConfigServiceTest` (fixture: `no_show` = 6; cross-key, 1000 unknown rows, overlapping reloads), `SystemConfigEditServiceTest`, `SystemConfigAdminServiceTest`, `SystemConfigAdminMapperTest`, `UpdateConfigRequestValidationTest`, `module/identity/SystemConfigAdminIT` (73 cases: contract shape vs SQL row by row, role matrix, BR-TK-17, every `ConfigKey` boundary, cross-key messages, malformed bodies, 405, failing triggers on `audit_logs` INSERT / `system_configs` UPDATE, reload failure via `@MockitoSpyBean SystemConfigRepository`, pair race with a `flush()` barrier — removing the partner lock leaves both commits and `reload()` then 500s — and same-key serialisation; `@AfterEach` restores the V2 values + `reload()`). **T14 part 5 (committed `6ed0962`, 2026-10-12, ADR-0035):** `GET /api/accounts` (#28), `POST /api/accounts/{id}/lock` (#29, Tài khoản#7), `/unlock` (#30, #8), `@PreAuthorize` ADMIN only (`AccountAdminController`; not through `StaffHierarchyPolicy`, wrong-role 403 not audited). #28 `AccountAdminQueryService` (readOnly, projection `AccountSummaryRow`, `q` in email with `StaffQueryService.likePattern`, `role` / `isLocked` filters, size 1–100, every role and status). Lock `AccountLockService.lockAccount` (one `@Transactional`, no facade, no `noRollbackFor`, catches nothing): self → 400 BR-QT-11 before any read → `findRoleById` projection (404) → if BRANCH_MANAGER `BranchApi.lockForStaffChange({branch read unlocked})` → `findByIdForUpdate` (404, e.g. ST02 deleted it meanwhile) → profile → BRANCH_MANAGER whose current branch is not in the lock map → 409 `ConcurrencyConflictException` → already locked → 409 → **read** pending work and recipients (`PendingWorkCollector`: A05–A08 any status → `VisitQueryApi.findUnfinishedVisitsAssignedTo` + `CashierShiftQueryApi.findOpenShift`; customer → `CustomerQueryApi` → `AppointmentQueryApi.findBookedByCustomer`, `BoardingQueryApi.findActiveStaysByCustomer`, `OrderQueryApi.findUnpaidOrdersByCustomer`; ADMIN / SUPER_MANAGER → none; a placeholder throws here → 500 with nothing written) → **write** `Account.lock(reason)` (`is_locked`, `locked_reason`, `last_seen_at = NULL`; `status` kept) → `revokeAll` → `publishEvent(AccountLockedEvent)` (no listener yet — D017, T26) → IN_APP `enqueue` (`AccountLockNotices`: A06–A08 → active BRANCH_MANAGERs of the branch, none → every SUPER_MANAGER, always sent; BRANCH_MANAGER → every SUPER_MANAGER, the **last** active manager of an `ACTIVE` branch read under the branch lock → one `LAST_BRANCH_MANAGER_LOCKED_APP` instead; customer → active receptionists of each branch with pending work, only that branch's items, none → WARN `ACCOUNT_LOCK_NO_RECIPIENT`; payload carries the reason) → audit `ACCOUNT_LOCKED` (before/after `{isLocked, status}`, `reason`) → flush → `LockResult{account, pendingWork}` sorted by enum order then `entityId` (`VISIT_IN_PROGRESS` = `needsReassign`). Unlock `unlockAccount`: `findByIdForUpdate` → not locked → 409 → `Account.unlock()` (only `is_locked`; `status` and `locked_reason` kept, BR-QT-12) → audit `ACCOUNT_UNLOCKED`; no branch lock, no session, no notice. `ReasonRequest.reason` `@NotBlank @Size(max = 500)`, stripped. Contract change for BE-2: `VisitQueryApi.findUnfinishedVisitIdsAssignedTo` → `findUnfinishedVisitsAssignedTo` (`AssignedVisit(visitId, branchId, status)`), `CashierShiftQueryApi.hasOpenShift` → `findOpenShift` (`Optional<OpenShift(shiftId, branchId)>`), `StaffAccountService.checkNoPendingWork` uses them (BR-QT-08 message unchanged); new `sales/service/OrderQueryApiPlaceholder` (no `OrderQueryApi` bean existed). `V12__seed_account_lock_templates.sql` (`ACCOUNT_LOCKED_FOLLOW_UP_APP`, `LAST_BRANCH_MANAGER_LOCKED_APP`). On the real app: lock / unlock ADMIN and SUPER_MANAGER, unlock of anyone and #28 work; locking A05–A08 (D016) or a customer (D010 + appointment / boarding / order placeholders) is 500 with nothing written. **T14 part 4 (committed `0f27381`, 2026-10-12, ADR-0034):** `POST /api/staff/{id}/email-change` (#23) and `/email-change/confirm` (#24), `@PreAuthorize` ADMIN / SUPER_MANAGER / BRANCH_MANAGER, facade `StaffAdminService.{startEmailChange, confirmEmailChange}` → **new bean** `StaffEmailChangeService` (`StaffAccountService` untouched: 3 unit tests build it with `new`). #23: `OtpService.prepare()` outside the tx → `issueEmailChangeOtp` (one `@Transactional`): `findByIdForUpdate` (404 missing / customer) → profile → policy `CHANGE_EMAIL` (403 audited after rollback) → normalize lower-case → same as current email → 400 BR-TK-01 "Email mới trùng email hiện tại" (Q5) → `existsByEmail` → 400 BR-TK-01 → `issuePrepared(CHANGE_EMAIL)` (BR-TK-07; invalidates the **account's** previous code — `OtpPurpose.invalidatesPerAccount()` is true for LINK_PROFILE and CHANGE_EMAIL, replaces the `customerId != null` test; `issueOtp` untouched) → outbox `OTP_EMAIL_CHANGE` to the new email; 200 `OtpSentResponse` (`maskedEmail` null); no audit (Q4). #24 (body only `{code}`): readOnly `findEmailChangeCode` (`findStaffRow` 404 → policy 403 **before** looking up the code → `OtpService.findActiveForAccount(id, CHANGE_EMAIL)` — new, CHANGE_EMAIL only, returns `AccountBoundOtp(id, targetEmail, codeHash)`; empty → facade 400 BR-TK-05) → BCrypt `matches` outside any tx → `applyEmailChange` (`noRollbackFor = OtpRejectedException`): lock `accounts` → profile → **policy re-checked under the lock** → `settleCheckedOtp` first write → `existsByEmail` (BR-TK-01 → rollback incl. `consumed_at`) → `Account.changeEmail` (only `email`) → `EMAIL_CHANGED_NOTICE` to old **and** new email → audit `STAFF_EMAIL_CHANGED` (before `{email}`, after `{email, verificationMethod: EMAIL_CODE}`) → `accounts.flush()` (`uq_accounts_email` → 409) → re-read projection. Any status (Q1), no session revoke (Q2), no `branches` lock. Accepted race R14: a login with the old email already past its unlocked read may succeed once. Debt D015 extended (quota per target email, unlocked count across accounts, Q7). No migration. **T14 part 3 (committed `c103a3b`, 2026-10-12, ADR-0033):** `POST /api/staff/{id}/disable` (#20, Tài khoản#5) and `/reactivate` (#21, #6), `@PreAuthorize` ADMIN / SUPER_MANAGER / BRANCH_MANAGER, same facade. Disable (`StaffAccountService.disableStaff`, one `@Transactional`): `lockTarget` (branch → `accounts` → profile) → policy `DISABLE` (403 audited) → A05–A08 without branch → 500 → BR-QT-12 (`is_locked`, any status) → **only if `ACTIVE` and A05–A08**: branch moved 409 → BR-QT-04 → BR-QT-08 last (SUPER_MANAGER never asked — Q2; placeholder → 500, D016) → `validateTransition` (**already DISABLED → 409**, Q1) → `Account.disable()` (`last_seen_at = NULL`) → `revokeAll` → audit `STAFF_DISABLED` (`{status}`) → flush → re-read; no email (Q3). Reactivate (`reactivateStaff`): facade `prepare()` temp password outside tx → `findByIdForUpdate` (404; **no `branches` lock** — only raises the manager count) → policy `REACTIVATE` (BM sending another branch → 403) → BR-QT-10 (`is_locked`) → BR-QT-03 (SUPER_MANAGER with `branchId`, unknown branch via `BranchQueryApi.findBranch`) → `validateTransition` (ACTIVE → 409) → `Account.reactivate(hash)` (ACTIVE, `must_change_password`, counters / `locked_until` / `last_seen_at` cleared) → optional `transferTo` → `revokeAll` → outbox `STAFF_TEMP_PASSWORD` → audit `STAFF_REACTIVATED` (`{status, branchId}`) → flush → re-read. Body of #21 optional (`@RequestBody(required = false)`; null / `{}` keeps the branch). `checkNoPendingWork(id, action)` now takes the message prefix. `AccountTransitionHandler.fsmName()` = "tài khoản" (409 message no longer leaks the class name). **D014 paid** (every flow lowering the ACTIVE BRANCH_MANAGER count locks the branch first; mutation removing it from disable → 5/5 race ITs red). No migration. **T14 part 2 (committed `b55b906`, 2026-10-12, ADR-0031):** `POST /api/staff/{id}/change-role` (#18) and `/transfer` (#19), `@PreAuthorize("hasRole('SUPER_MANAGER')")`, through the same facade (`StaffAdminService`, no tx, audits 403 after rollback) → `StaffAccountService.changeStaffRole` / `transferStaff` (one `@Transactional`, no `noRollbackFor`): `lockTarget` = `findStaffRow` projection (missing / customer → 404) → `BranchApi.lockForStaffChange({current branch} ∪ {destination})` → `findByIdForUpdate(accounts)` → read `staff_profiles` → policy (403 audited) → A05–A08 without branch → 500 → **same value → 200, no write, no audit** (checked before the conflict check, so a double-click that already happened while waiting gets 200) → branch changed while waiting → 409 `ConcurrencyConflictException` → (#19) destination absent from the lock map → 400 BR-QT-03 → BR-QT-04 (target BRANCH_MANAGER `ACTIVE` + branch read under lock `ACTIVE` + `countActiveBranchManagers ≤ 1`; source branch for #19) → (#19) BR-QT-08 **last**, both `VisitQueryApi` and `CashierShiftQueryApi` for every role, message lists everything (placeholders → transfer 500 until 21/10, D016) → `Account.changeStaffRole` (A05–A08 only) [+ `StaffProfile.clearVetProfile()` when leaving VET] / `StaffProfile.transferTo` → audit `STAFF_ROLE_CHANGED` (`before`/`after` = `role, specialty, bio`, `LinkedHashMap` because values may be null) / `STAFF_TRANSFERRED` (`branchId`) → `flush()` → re-read `findStaffDetail`. User decisions: DISABLED / locked targets allowed (Q1), same value no-op (Q2), VET leaving VET clears specialty/bio (Q3), change-role does **not** check pending work (Q4, TBD before T26); no session revoke, no email. Obligation (ADR-0031 §9, 06 §4, D016): assigning a visit (T26) / opening a cashier shift (BE-2) must `SELECT … FROM accounts WHERE id = <staff> FOR SHARE` and re-check branch/role/status under it — FK checks only take `FOR KEY SHARE`, which does not conflict with transfer's `FOR NO KEY UPDATE`. **ADR-0032 (same day):** `spring.jackson.deserialization.accept-float-as-int: false` in `application.yml` — `{"branchId": 1.9}` used to become `1` silently; now any float into an integer field is 400 `MALFORMED_REQUEST` (whole app, BE-2 told via 06 §8 Q5; `BigDecimal`/`double` unaffected). Contract #18/#19 gained 409 + notes (generator). No migration. **T14 part 1 (committed `90dfc6c`, 2026-10-11, ADR-0030 — first way to create staff without SQL):** `StaffController` `/api/staff` (identity-v1 #15 list, #16 create, #17 get, #22 `POST {id}/resend-initial-password`; `@PreAuthorize` ADMIN / SUPER_MANAGER / BRANCH_MANAGER). Create/resend go through facade `StaffAdminService` (**not** `@Transactional`: `TemporaryPasswordGenerator.prepare()` BCrypt outside the tx; catches `StaffHierarchyDeniedException` after rollback → `recordIndependently` → rethrow) → `StaffAccountService` (one `@Transactional`). Create: policy → BR-QT-03 (SUPER_MANAGER with `branchId` → 400; A05–A08 without branch / unknown branch → 400; BRANCH_MANAGER null = own branch; no `branches` lock) → BR-TK-01 → `validateInitial(ACTIVE)` → `Account.createStaff` + `StaffProfile.create` (`save` merges: PK assigned) → outbox `STAFF_TEMP_PASSWORD` `{ho_ten, email_dang_nhap, mat_khau_tam}` → audit `STAFF_CREATED` (`after` = email, phone, fullName, role, branchId, status — **no key containing `password`**, `AuditRecorder` splits camelCase and rejects it) → `flush()` → re-read projection. Request `role` is a `String` + `@Pattern` (so ADMIN/CUSTOMER/garbage = 400 VALIDATION_FAILED on field `role`). Resend: `findByIdForUpdate` first → 404 (missing / customer) → read `staff_profiles` → policy (self denied) → 409 `InvalidStateTransitionException` if `DISABLED` or `must_change_password = false` (`is_locked` allowed) → `Account.reissueInitialPassword` (hash; clears counter, `locked_until`, `last_seen_at`) → `SessionService.revokeAll` → outbox → audit `STAFF_INITIAL_PASSWORD_RESENT`, 204. View (`StaffQueryService`, readOnly, 403 not audited): ADMIN sees SUPER_MANAGER, SUPER_MANAGER sees A05–A08, BRANCH_MANAGER sees A06–A08 of own branch; `role` filter outside scope → empty page; BRANCH_MANAGER other `branchId` → 403; get: missing/customer → 404 same message. `StaffResponse` (12 contract fields, `isLocked` = `is_locked` only) built only by `StaffAdminMapper` from projection `StaffDetailRow` (secrets never SELECTed); `online` via package-private `StaffPresence` (same formula as `StaffDirectoryService`, which was left untouched; `StaffPresenceParityTest`). Side effects by design: a BRANCH_MANAGER created by API makes the branch activatable; a new VET shows on `/api/public/vets` immediately. Obligation for parts 3/5: set `last_seen_at = NULL` when revoking sessions. No migration. **T14 part 0 (committed `90dfc6c`, 2026-10-11, ADR-0029 — staff-administration foundation, no endpoint):** `identity/service/StaffHierarchyPolicy` (pure, not `@Transactional`; ADMIN → SUPER_MANAGER, SUPER_MANAGER → A05–A08, BRANCH_MANAGER → A06–A08 of own branch, `branchId` null on create = own branch; change-role/transfer SUPER_MANAGER only, also `@PreAuthorize`; self always denied) + `StaffAction`; denial throws `identity/exception/StaffHierarchyDeniedException extends AccessDeniedScopeException` (403 generic message) carrying `AuditEntry` `STAFF_ACTION_DENIED` (`QtAuditActions`) — the facade of parts 1–5 must `recordIndependently` it **after** the business tx rolled back; check order 400 → 403 role → 404 → 403 hierarchy → 400 BR → 409; lock order `branches (id ↑) → accounts → staff_profiles → sessions`, every read before a lock is a projection; `branch/api/BranchApi.lockForStaffChange` + `branch/service/BranchStaffLockService` (BE-1 in BE-2's module: `MANDATORY`, rejects `readOnly`, native scalar `SELECT status … FOR NO KEY UPDATE` via `BranchRepository.lockStatusForStaffChange` — `findByIdForUpdate` would return a stale `Branch` already loaded by `BranchQueryApi.findBranch`; ids locked ascending; unknown ids absent; D014 step 1); `identity/service/TemporaryPasswordGenerator.prepare()` (outside tx, `max(12, password.min_length)` chars, letter + digit, no `0 O o 1 l I`, record components `plainPassword`/`passwordHash` so `AuditRecorder` rejects it); `visit/service/VisitQueryApiPlaceholder`, `sales/service/CashierShiftQueryApiPlaceholder` (debt D016: transfer/disable will 500 until 21/10); `V11__seed_staff_admin_templates.sql` (`STAFF_TEMP_PASSWORD`, `OTP_EMAIL_CHANGE`, `EMAIL_CHANGED_NOTICE`; **V11 confirmed with BE-2 on 2026-10-12**). Earlier, **task 08 part 4 (committed `ec8b639`, 2026-10-10, ADR-0028 — BE-1 builds in BE-2's `customer` module):** UC06 for customers, customer-v1 #1–7: `GET/PATCH /api/me/customer-profile` and `/api/me/addresses` (list, add 201, patch, delete 204, `POST {id}/set-default`) in `module/customer` — first entities of that module, `Customer` (every `customers` column) + `Address` (`is_default` ↔ field `defaultAddress`, `@JsonProperty("isDefault")` on the record), `MyCustomerController` (CUSTOMER only, not blocked by the BR-TK-19 pending flag), `MyCustomerProfileService`, `AddressBookService`, `MyCustomerLookup`; every write first locks the owner's `customers` row (`FOR NO KEY UPDATE` by `account_id`; lock order `customers → addresses`, never `accounts`; if the lock query finds no row — UC07 replaced the profile while it waited — re-read the id with a new statement and lock by id); BR-TK-18 limit `address.max_per_customer` [CFG] counted under that lock; first address always default; `AddressRepository.clearDefault` (native bulk UPDATE, `flushAutomatically`, sets `updated_at`) runs **before** the INSERT/UPDATE of the new default because Hibernate flushes INSERT before UPDATE and `uq_addresses_default_per_customer` is not deferrable; deleting the default while others exist → 400 BR-TK-18; hard delete of addresses (erd §0 now lists BR-TK-18); someone else's address → 404 same message; PATCH profile: `email` sent → 400 BR-TK-15 before any read, null keeps / blank clears, COUNTER profile clearing phone → 400 BR-KH-01; response `email` = the **account** email from the principal (not `customers.email`); request phones `^0\d{9}$`, avatar `https://` only, response keeps the generic types; Bean Validation runs before `@PreAuthorize` (staff with a bad body gets 400); no audit; `V10__address_customer_index.sql` (`ix_addresses_customer_id`, V10 confirmed with BE-2 2026-10-10) pays the `addresses` part of D013. `CustomerApi` / `CustomerQueryApi` stay placeholders (D001, D010). Lock order `accounts → otp_tokens → customers`. `customer.api` changed: `linkAccountToCounterProfile` signature (no `actorId`, adds `expectedCounterEmail`), new `checkOnlineProfileLinkable` + enum `OnlineProfileLinkability`; customer must delete the online profile's `addresses`, then the profile, then `flush()` before setting `C.account_id` (`uq_customers_account_id` is not deferrable, Hibernate flushes UPDATE before DELETE). staff → `staff_profiles` + `accounts.phone`, a staff account **without** a `staff_profiles` row is bad data → `IllegalStateException` 500; customer → `CustomerQueryApi.findCustomerIdByAccountId` → `findContact(...).linkDecisionPending()`, placeholder → 500, D010) → `MeMapper` (MapStruct, reuses `LoginMapper.toAccountSummary`, `unmappedTargetPolicy = ERROR`); `countBranchManagers` counts `status = ACTIVE` BRANCH_MANAGERs **including locked ones**; "active" = `ACTIVE` and not `is_locked`; online = `last_seen_at` within `staff.online_window_minutes` [CFG]; `listPublicVets` does not filter `ACTIVE` branches — content does), `OtpService` has `customerId` overloads for `LINK_PROFILE` (ADR-0025: lookup by account + email + customer, a new link code invalidates only the same account's link codes, guard purpose ⇔ `customerId`), `V9__otp_profile_link.sql` (template `OTP_PROFILE_LINK` + partial index `otp_tokens(customer_id)`); Schema: `V1__init_schema.sql` (all 55 ERD tables) + `V2__seed_system_configs.sql` (52 [CFG] rows) + `V3__seed_notification_template_otp_register.sql` (first template, `OTP_REGISTER`) + `V4__st20_notification_outbox.sql` (outbox lane index, `OTP_REGISTER` greeting `Xin chào,`) + `V5__account_pending_expires_at.sql` (`accounts.pending_expires_at` snapshot for ST02, `ck_accounts_pending_expiry`, index `otp_tokens(account_id)`) + `V6__in_app_notification_index.sql` (partial index `ix_notification_outbox_in_app_due` for the ST20 IN_APP lane) + `V7__account_fk_indexes.sql` (38 indexes, one per FK column to `accounts` that had none usable — ADR-0018, D009 paid) + `V8__seed_notification_templates_login_password.sql` (templates `OTP_PASSWORD_RESET`, `LOGIN_LOCKED_WARNING`, `PASSWORD_CHANGED` for task 07 — ADR-0019) + `V9__otp_profile_link.sql` (template `OTP_PROFILE_LINK`, index `ix_otp_tokens_customer_id` — ADR-0025). `module/<m>/api/` holds the cross-module contracts of all 11 modules (interfaces/records/events only, `docs/06-module-contracts.md`); implemented module code: `module/identity` — session authentication, principal, BR-TK-17 interceptor, `SystemConfigApi` (ADR-0003, ADR-0004), the first scheduled job, `SessionCleanupJob` (ADR-0007, ADR-0008), and the **first business endpoints, `POST /api/auth/register`** (UC01: `AuthController` → `RegistrationService`, `OtpService`, `AccountTransitionHandler`; ADR-0009 BCrypt) **and `POST /api/auth/register/verify`** (UC02, Tài khoản#2: `RegistrationService.verifyAccount` → `OtpService.consumeOtp`; a wrong code returns 400 yet the `failed_attempts` increment commits — ADR-0010) **and `POST /api/auth/register/resend-otp`** (UC02, 2026-10-07: `RegistrationService.resendRegistrationOtp` → `OtpService.issueOtp`; payload without `ten_khach`). Every OTP flow first locks the `accounts` row with `AccountRepository.findByEmailForUpdate` (`SELECT … FOR UPDATE`, ADR-0011; D005 paid). `care` has `NotificationService` (implements `NotificationApi`, writes `notification_outbox`) and the **ST20 email worker** (2026-10-07, ADR-0012: `PriorityNotificationJob` every 5 s for `OTP_*`/`STAFF_TEMP_PASSWORD`, `NotificationOutboxJob` every 10 s for the rest, both via `NotificationOutboxRunner` → `NotificationDispatchService` — the NORMAL lane sends **batches of 20 over one SMTP connection** through `SmtpBatchSender` (ADR-0017, D008 paid); templates read through `identity.api.NotificationTemplateQueryApi`) and the **ST20 IN_APP lane** (2026-10-07, ADR-0014, D006 paid: `InAppNotificationJob` every 5 s → `InAppNotificationDeliveryService` turns each `IN_APP` outbox row into exactly one `notifications` row in the same transaction; no UC88 endpoint yet) and **`NotificationOutboxCleanupJob`** (ADR-0016, D007 paid: daily 03:40, deletes `SENT` rows older than 30 days and `FAILED` older than 90 by `created_at`, never `PENDING`). `customer` has the customer self-service endpoints of task 08 part 4 (ADR-0028) but still only `CustomerApiPlaceholder` and `CustomerQueryApiPlaceholder` for its `api/` (both throw; the latter makes customer login return 500 once login exists — debt D010, staff login unaffected), so register (and verify at its `flagLinkDecisionIfPhoneMatches` step) returns 500 on the real app until BE-2 delivers `CustomerApi` — debt D001 (resend does not call customer). **ST02** (2026-10-07, ADR-0013, D002 paid): `identity/job/PendingAccountCleanupJob` every 15 min deletes `PENDING` accounts whose `pending_expires_at` (= registration time + `account.pending_ttl_hours` [CFG], snapshotted at register, cleared by verify) has passed — one account per transaction in `PendingAccountCleanupService.purge` (`FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` + re-check → `otp_tokens` → `CustomerApi.deleteOnlineProfileOfUnverifiedAccount` → `accounts`), 60 s time budget per run, WARN `PENDING_ACCOUNT_CLEANUP_SLOW` (FK checks on `accounts` are indexed since V7); verify/resend still do not check the expiry themselves. **`POST /api/auth/login`** (UC03 + ST01, 2026-10-08, ADR-0019): `AuthController.login` → `LoginService` (**not** `@Transactional`: reads `(id, password_hash)` in a short readOnly tx, runs BCrypt while holding no connection, writes the 400-branch audit after rollback) → `LoginAttemptService.login` (`@Transactional(noRollbackFor = InvalidCredentialsException.class)`: `findByIdForUpdate`, guards temp-lock → password → PENDING/locked/DISABLED, `Account.recordFailedLogin` fixed window + `locked_until` + `LOGIN_LOCKED_WARNING` email, session, audit); every 401 has the same message (BR-TK-10); customer login reads `linkDecisionPending` via the throwing `CustomerQueryApiPlaceholder` → 500 until BE-2 (D010). Open Session In View is **off** (`spring.jpa.open-in-view: false`, ADR-0020). **`POST /api/auth/logout`** (UC03, 2026-10-08, ADR-0021): `AuthController.logout` → `LogoutService.logout` (one `@Transactional`: `AccountRepository.clearLastSeen` — waits for the row lock, no `SKIP LOCKED` — **then** `SessionService.revoke`; lock order `accounts → sessions`), 204 without body, body ignored, any role, exempt from BR-TK-17, no audit; `touchLastSeen` now also requires the request's session to be unrevoked (`EXISTS`), so an in-flight request with the same token cannot mark a logged-out staff member online. **`POST /api/me/password`** (UC05, BR-TK-14/17, 2026-10-08, ADR-0022): `MeController` → `ChangePasswordService` (**not** `@Transactional`: reads `(password_hash, locked_until)` readOnly, temporarily locked → 400 BR-TK-09 without BCrypt, BCrypt `matches` + `NewPasswordHasher` (`PasswordPolicy` BR-TK-03 → not same as current → `encode`) while holding no connection) → `ChangePasswordAttemptService.apply` (`@Transactional(noRollbackFor = CurrentPasswordMismatchException.class)`: `findByIdForUpdate`, guard BR-TK-11/09, wrong → `Account.recordFailedLogin` — **the login counter, shared** — + on lock `LOGIN_LOCKED_WARNING` + audit `ACCOUNT_TEMPORARILY_LOCKED`, 400 BR-TK-14; right → `Account.changePassword` (hash, `must_change_password = false`, counter reset) then `SessionService.revokeOthers`); 204, no email, no audit on success. `PasswordPolicy` (BR-TK-03) is shared with registration. **`POST /api/auth/password/forgot` + `/reset`** (UC04, BR-TK-03…07/10/12/13, 2026-10-08, ADR-0023): `AuthController` → `PasswordResetService` (**not** `@Transactional`; every BCrypt runs here with no connection or row lock — public endpoint, BCrypt under the lock would let ~10 parallel requests to one email drain the pool) → `PasswordResetAttemptService`. Forgot: `OtpService.prepare()` (code + BCrypt, every branch) → `issueResetOtp` (`findByEmailForUpdate`, only `ACTIVE` and not `is_locked`, temporarily locked still OK → `OtpService.issuePrepared` → outbox `OTP_PASSWORD_RESET`) → **always 202 with the same body** (`ApiResponse.accepted`, MapStruct `PasswordResetMapper`); BR-TK-07 and `PessimisticLockingFailureException` are caught in the facade **after** rollback (catching inside → `UnexpectedRollbackException` 500; letting them out → 400/409 reveals the email). Reset: `PasswordPolicy` → readOnly `findResetCandidate` → exactly 2 `matches` outside tx (`BcryptDecoy` when no candidate) → `applyReset` (`@Transactional(noRollbackFor = OtpRejectedException.class)`: `findByIdForUpdate` → `OtpService.settleCheckedOtp` (newer code than the one read or expired → BR-TK-05 not counted; wrong → counter committed) → same-as-current BR-TK-03 only after a correct code (rollback keeps the code usable) → `Account.resetPassword` (also clears `locked_until`, `must_change_password`, `last_seen_at`) → `revokeAll` → outbox `PASSWORD_CHANGED`); unknown / ineligible / codeless email → the same 400 BR-TK-05; 204, no audit. `GET /api/me`, `PATCH /api/me/staff-profile`, `GET /api/public/vets` (task 08 parts 1–2) and `/api/me/link-*` (part 3) exist; staff management: create / list / get / resend initial password (T14 part 1), change role / transfer (T14 part 2; transfer 500 until D016), disable / reactivate (T14 part 3; disabling A05–A08 500 until D016), email change (T14 part 4) and account search / lock / unlock (T14 part 5; locking A05–A08 or customers 500 until D016 / D010) exist. **`catalog`** (2026-10-08, UC28–UC31): the 18 staff endpoints of catalog-v1 (`/api/product-categories`, `/products`, `/services`, `/vaccine-types`, `/vaccination-protocols`; read = 5 staff roles, write = SUPER_MANAGER only) with BR-SP-01/02/04/05/06/07 and `CatalogQueryService implements CatalogQueryApi`; the 4 public endpoints (#19–22, UC15/16) are not built. `StockQueryApiPlaceholder` (inventory) and `VaccinationQueryApiPlaceholder` (visit) throw, so turning off `tracksExpiry` (BR-SP-05) and deleting a vaccine type (BR-SP-07) return 500 on the real app until those modules ship; ITs mock both interfaces (`CatalogAdminIT`). Team split: BE-1 = identity, appointment, visit, care; BE-2 = branch, customer, catalog, sales, inventory, boarding, content, report; dates in `docs/timeline_petcare_v15.xlsx`. `docs/architecture/system-overview.md` is the detailed, file-by-file snapshot of what exists — update it when adding a module or infrastructure.

Team: **BE-1** (branch `manh`) = identity, appointment, visit, care · **BE-2** (branch `dung`) = branch, customer, catalog, sales, inventory, boarding, content, report · **FE-1** = all UI. PRs go to `dev`; `main` is the default branch. Phase 1 runs 05–30/10/2026 (tier 1–2 scope), phase 2 is Nov–Dec (tier 3).

## Business in one page (spec v16)

Source of truth is `docs/01`–`05`; start at `docs/INDEX.md`. It maps every module to the exact line ranges in each spec file.

**Actors:** A01 GUEST · A02 CUSTOMER · A03 ADMIN (technical only: SUPER_MANAGER accounts, lock / unlock, [CFG], templates, audit) · A04 SUPER_MANAGER (whole chain) · A05 BRANCH_MANAGER, A06 RECEPTIONIST (also cashier), A07 VET, A08 CARETAKER — each of A05–A08 belongs to exactly one branch.

**Main flow:** book appointment (LH) → check-in creates a **Visit** + opens an **Order** with one auto line (TN) → exam / vaccination / grooming (KB) → Visit completed → Order `PENDING` → cashier collects in an open shift (TG) → stock deducted FEFO (KO) → vaccination / follow-up reminders and Care Tasks (TB). Boarding (LT) runs in parallel and ends in a `BOARDING` Order paid before the pet is handed back.

**Cross-cutting principles (04 §Nguyên tắc):**
1. **Customer is the root, Account is optional.** Business data points to `customers`, never to `accounts`; linking an account = setting `customers.account_id` (BR-TK-19).
2. **Identity = verified email only.** Phone is never unique. CCCD (national ID) is never stored (BR-TK-01, 16, BR-KH-10).
3. **No hard delete** except where a rule allows it: BR-KH-06, BR-TK-08, BR-TK-18, BR-TK-19, BR-BV-02, BR-KB-04. The app deletes children itself (FKs are `RESTRICT`).
4. **Price snapshots:** an order line freezes its price when added; a boarding booking freezes its nightly rate.
5. **All stock is per lot.** Every quantity change writes a `stock_movements` row; FEFO never issues expired lots.
6. **Cross-aggregate consequences run in one transaction**, coordinated by the aggregate that emits the event (03 *Phụ lục*).
7. **Vaccines are counted by `VaccineType`**, not by product brand.
8. **Only vaccinations recorded by the system are trusted.** External history is free text in the medical record.
9. **Branch scope** applies to data with `branch_id`. Customers, pets, catalogs and suppliers are chain-wide.
10. **[CFG] values apply only to work created after the change** (BR-QT-13): snapshot them into the row.

**Key cross-object chains (03 *Phụ lục*), all in one transaction:**

| Event | Consequences |
|---|---|
| Visit#1 check-in | Appointment → `CHECKED_IN`; Order (VISIT) `OPEN` + auto line |
| Visit#5 complete | Appointment → `COMPLETED`; Order → `PENDING`; medical record locked |
| Visit#6 cancel | Appointment → `CANCELLED` (not late); Order → `CANCELLED` |
| Vaccination recorded / deleted | FEFO deduct / restore to the same lot; vaccine order line added / removed; old Care Task cancelled |
| Order#8 paid | FEFO deduct; payment recorded in the shift |
| Pet deceased / owner transferred | `BOOKED` appointments and bookings → `CANCELLED` (not late); reminders / Care Tasks cancelled or moved |

**Scope tiers:** 1 = full, 2 = thin, 3 = diagram only — **don't build tier 3**; the list is in `docs/INDEX.md`. Rules marked 🆕 are proposals not yet approved. Vietnamese enum values map to ASCII codes (erd §0, e.g. `TÁI_CHỦNG` → `VACCINE_DUE`, nhóm Khám/Tiêm → `MEDICAL`).

## Commands

All Maven commands run from `BE/`; npm commands from `FE/`.

```bash
cp .env.example .env                              # once (repo root): compose has no JWT_SECRET default
docker compose up -d postgres redis mailpit       # local deps; Mailpit SMTP :1025, UI http://localhost:8025
docker compose up -d --build                      # full stack smoke test (BE on 8080 in Docker)

mvn clean compile -DskipTests
mvn spring-boot:run                               # port 8081 (SERVER_PORT)
mvn test                                          # Surefire: *Test / *Tests
mvn verify                                        # + Failsafe: *IT
mvn test -Dtest=SomeServiceTest#someMethod
mvn verify -Dit.test=SomeFlowIT -Dtest=none -Dsurefire.failIfNoSpecifiedTests=false

npm ci && npm run dev                             # http://localhost:5173, /api proxied to VITE_API_URL or :8080
npm run build                                     # tsc && vite build
npm run lint                                      # eslint --max-warnings 0
npx tsc --noEmit                                  # no typecheck script
npm run test / npm run test:e2e                   # Vitest / Playwright

python docs/api/generator/generate.py && node docs/api/check-contracts.mjs   # after editing an API contract
```

Tests need Docker running (Testcontainers `postgres:17`, real Flyway migrations, `ddl-auto=validate`). Surefire and Failsafe pin `-Duser.timezone=Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh`.

## Documentation map

Never invent a rule, transition, model, column or enum value. When something is missing, record it as an assumption or TBD (in the ADR, a debt file, or contract §E).

| Need | File | Priority on conflict |
|---|---|---|
| Business rules `BR-<MODULE>-<n>` | `docs/02-business-rules.md` | 1 (highest) |
| State machines, numbered transitions (`Visit#5`) | `docs/03-state-machines.md` | 2 |
| Domain model (ROOT / PART / REF / LOG) | `docs/04-domain-model.md` | 3 |
| ERD: 55 tables, enum codes, decision log §13 | `docs/05-erd.md` | 4 |
| Actors, UC01–UC89, ST01–ST21 | `docs/01-business-operations.md` | must match rules |
| BE ↔ BE contracts (`module/<m>/api`), placeholder notes for the other dev | `docs/06-module-contracts.md` | |
| FE ↔ BE HTTP contracts (12 modules, 230 endpoints) | `docs/api/*-v1.{md,yaml}` — **generated** from `docs/api/generator/m_<module>.py`; never hand-edit | |
| Backend conventions 01–10 (+ open TBDs in `INDEX.md`) | `docs/convention/backend/` | |
| Manual FE test cases / task plans | `docs/testcases/`, `docs/plan/` | |

Module codes: TK, QT, CN, CK, KH, SP, NS, LH, TN, KB, LT, BH, TG, KO, BV, DG, TB, BC.

The numbers 0041–0044 are used twice (BE-1 and BE-2) and still need renumbering. Next free number: 0053.

## Backend architecture

```
com.petcare/
├── platform/         # cross-cutting infra; must NOT import module/*
│                     # audit, config, exception, fsm, model (envelopes), security, sequence
└── module/<m>/       # api/ controller/ service/ repository/ entity/ dto/ mapper/ [fsm/] [exception/] [job/] [listener/]
```

- **Module boundary:** a module imports only another module's `api/` package (interfaces, records, enums, events). It never imports another module's `entity`, `repository` or internal `service`.
- **1–1 consequence** = interface call inside the caller's transaction (implementation is `@Transactional(MANDATORY)`; the caller never catches its exceptions).
- **Fan-out consequence** = synchronous event (`ApplicationEventPublisher` + `@EventListener` in `module/<m>/listener/`, delegating to a `MANDATORY` service). Not `AFTER_COMMIT`, no `@Async`, no `REQUIRES_NEW`, no swallowed exceptions. A listener failure rolls back the publisher.
- **Unbuilt interface:** the owner commits an `<Interface>Placeholder` that throws `UnsupportedOperationException`, never a "safe" default. Callers mock it in tests.
- **Request flow:** `Controller` (`@RequestMapping("/api/…")`, `@Valid`, `@PreAuthorize`, no logic, never sees a rule id) → `Service` (`@Transactional` spanning one use case, rules, `BranchScope`) → `[TransitionHandler]` → `Repository`.
  - Entities never leave the service. Mapping uses MapStruct (`componentModel = "spring"`, `unmappedTargetPolicy = ERROR`); DTOs are records.
  - Bean Validation runs before `@PreAuthorize`. Every constraint carries a Vietnamese `message`.
- **Success envelope:** `ApiResponse<T>{data, message, code}` via `ok`, `created`, `accepted`; lists put `PageResponse<T>` inside `data`. A 204 is a `void` handler + `@ResponseStatus(NO_CONTENT)`.
- **Error envelope:** `ErrorResponse{success:false, errorCode, message, statusCode, timestamp, traceId}`, produced only by `GlobalExceptionHandler`. Exception → HTTP status:

  | Exception | Status |
  |---|---|
  | `BusinessRuleViolationException(ruleId, msg)` | 400 (appends ` (BR-…)` itself) |
  | `ResourceNotFoundException` | 404 |
  | `AccessDeniedScopeException` | 403 |
  | `InvalidStateTransitionException`, `ConcurrencyConflictException`, DB lock / constraint errors | 409 |

  **No 422.** Subclass an exception only for ≥ 2 extra fields, a different status, or special retry semantics.
- **FSM:** enum + `{Entity}TransitionHandler extends StateMachineBase<S>`. `allowedTransitions()` is copied **exactly** from 03 — never add an edge by inference. Rule guards run before `validateTransition()`. Article (BR-BV-02) and Feedback (BR-DG-04) are rule-only lifecycles. The account lock is the `is_locked` flag, independent of `status`.
- **Error precedence** is always: 401 → 400 BR-TK-17 → 400 malformed / validation → 403 role → data authorization / 404. After that, the order is decided per feature in its ADR: staff admin puts 400 BR before 409 (ADR-0029); appointment puts 409 before any 400 (ADR-0045). Copy the neighbouring feature.

### Platform toolbox (reuse, don't re-create)

| Need | Use |
|---|---|
| Time | Inject `Clock` (`TimeConfig.BUSINESS_ZONE` = Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh): `Instant.now(clock)`, `LocalDate.now(clock)`. Never call `now()` without the clock. `created_at` / `updated_at` also come from the clock (`TimestampedEntity` / `CreatedAtEntity`, ADR-0015); bulk updates set `updated_at` themselves |
| Current user / scope | `BranchScope.current()` (accountId, role, branchId, `CHAIN` / `BRANCH` / `OWNER`), `resolve(branchId)` for lists, `check(branchId)` for a loaded row. CUSTOMER always gets 403 there: filter customer data by owner instead (ADR-0006). Call it only for data that has `branch_id` |
| Auth | JWT HS256 carries only `sub`, `sid`, `jti`; the `sessions` row is re-checked on every request; role, branch and `mustChangePassword` come from the DB. Public paths (`SecurityConfig.PUBLIC_PATHS`) are always anonymous. Open / revoke sessions with `SessionService` (`MANDATORY`). BR-TK-17 is enforced by `MustChangePasswordInterceptor` |
| [CFG] | `identity.api.SystemConfigApi.getInt/getDecimal/getBool/getTime(ConfigKey.X)`. A new key = new `ConfigKey` + seed migration; startup fails on a missing or invalid key or a broken cross-key pair (`ConfigConstraints`). Never hard-code a [CFG] number |
| Audit (BR-QT-15/16) | `AuditRecorder.record(AuditEntry.of("ACTION").entity(t, id).before(..).after(..).reason(..))` inside the use-case tx. `recordIndependently` (REQUIRES_NEW) only for failures that must survive rollback, called **after** the rollback. Snapshots are records / Maps serialising to a JSON object; keys containing password / otp / token / secret / cccd / pin are rejected. `audit_logs` is insert-only (trigger) |
| Notifications | `care.api.NotificationApi.enqueue(NotificationRequest(templateCode, channel, accountId, email, payload, link))` in the same tx (inserts into `notification_outbox` only). EMAIL needs a snapshotted email; IN_APP needs an account id that is not `PENDING`. A new template = seed migration in the same PR; its text must pass the strict `{var}` grammar (ADR-0037). Payload keys are the stable contract. Secret keys (`otp`, `mat_khau`, `token`…) are scrubbed after sending. `enqueue` does not check `required_vars` — check them yourself |
| Passwords / OTP | `PasswordEncoder` (BCrypt 10). Inputs over 72 bytes are BR-TK-03, checked before encode **and** before `matches`. `OtpService` (ADR-0010/0011/0023/0025): lock `accounts` first; every tx an `OtpRejectedException` passes through needs `noRollbackFor`; on public endpoints use `prepare` / `issuePrepared` / `findActive` / `settleCheckedOtp` so BCrypt never runs under a row lock |
| Document codes | `platform/sequence/DocumentCodeGenerator` (gap-free, table `document_counters`); appointments use sequence `appointment_code_seq` (gaps allowed) |
| Swagger | springdoc. Each handler gets `@Operation` copied verbatim from `docs/api/openapi/<m>-v1.yaml`, plus `@ApiErrorDoc` per 4xx and `@Parameter`; every DTO field gets `@Schema(description, example)`. Never write `security` / 401 / 500. `OpenApiContractParityIT` must stay green (ADR-0040, convention 10) |
| JSON | A float into an integer field → 400 `MALFORMED_REQUEST` (ADR-0032) |
| Jobs | `module/<m>/job/{Name}Job` with `@Scheduled(cron = "${app.jobs.<name>.cron}", zone = BUSINESS_ZONE_ID)`. Not `@Transactional`; catch and log `<NAME>_FAILED`, never rethrow; MDC `traceId`; cron `"-"` in `application-test.yml`; cron as an env var in `docker-compose.yml` / `.env.example`. Pool = 4 threads, single instance. Existing jobs: `SessionCleanupJob`, `PendingAccountCleanupJob` (ST02, every 15 min), `PriorityNotificationJob` / `NotificationOutboxJob` / `InAppNotificationJob` (ST20), `NotificationOutboxCleanupJob`, `NoShowJob` (ST05, `0 */5`), `AppointmentReminderJob` (ST03, `0 2/5`) |

## Transactions, locking, concurrency — house style
>>>>>>> af117e0 (feat: triển khai job nhắc lịch)

Every write ADR follows these. Read the ADR of the neighbouring feature before writing a new flow.

- **One `@Transactional` per use case** on the service. No `noRollbackFor` except for a committed failure counter (OTP, login, current password). Such exceptions must be thrown directly inside that method, after its writes. Never `catch` inside the tx: it becomes rollback-only and fails later with `UnexpectedRollbackException`.
- **Facade without a tx** when work must happen outside it:
  - BCrypt (never hold a connection or a row lock during it);
  - an audit after rollback (403 hierarchy denial, failed login);
  - a cache reload after commit;
  - catching an error that must not leak (forgot password always returns 202).
- **Order inside a use case:**
  1. Authorize first (role, then ownership or scope on an immutable projection) — before any lock.
  2. Lock.
  3. Read under the lock. Never `findById` before locking: the persistence context returns the stale entity. Lock with a native scalar query or a projection.
  4. Take `now = clock.instant()` after the locks (truncate to µs when the value is returned and re-read).
  5. Guards.
  6. Write.
  7. `saveAndFlush` / `flush`, so DB errors surface inside the call.
  8. Audit and outbox.
  9. Map.
- `findByIdForUpdate` emits **`FOR NO KEY UPDATE`** (Hibernate 6). FK checks only take `FOR KEY SHARE`, which does **not** conflict with it: a module inserting a row that points at a staff member or a pet must first lock that row `FOR SHARE` (`PetQueryApi.findPetForShare`, `accounts … FOR SHARE` for visit assignment / shift opening).
- Put `@Transactional(MANDATORY)` on repository lock methods too. Otherwise a lock called outside a tx locks and releases immediately in autocommit.
- **Lock orders (never reverse):**

  | Area | Order |
  |---|---|
  | Sessions | `accounts → sessions` (revoke via `@Modifying` queries in that order) |
  | OTP | `accounts → otp_tokens → customers` |
  | Staff admin | `branches (id ↑, only via BranchApi.lockForStaffChange) → accounts → staff_profiles → sessions`. Any flow lowering a branch's ACTIVE BRANCH_MANAGER count locks the branch first |
  | Customer | `accounts → customers (id ↑) → pets → weight_records` |
  | Appointment writes | `advisory(1001, pet) → branches (BranchApi.lockForBooking) → appointments`; advisory **1002** per customer (violation evaluation / restriction lift) always last |
  | Visit (T26, obligations of ADR-0046 / BE-2's 0044) | visit's own advisory / `branches` / `visits` locks → `appointments` row via `AppointmentApi` → `orders` via `VisitOrderApi` → stock lots via `StockApi` |
  | Sales | caller's document → `orders` (lock before read) → `products FOR SHARE` → stock lots; several orders by ascending id |
  | Inventory | caller's document → `stock_receipts` → `suppliers FOR SHARE` → `products FOR SHARE` → all lots in **one** `ORDER BY id FOR NO KEY UPDATE` → `StockLedger.apply` (the only writer of `stock_lots.quantity`) |
  | [CFG] edit | `system_configs` rows by ascending key, the key and its cross-key partner |

- **Isolation:** default READ COMMITTED. Paged reads (count + page) use `readOnly` + REPEATABLE READ. A write that re-reads after an advisory lock must stay READ COMMITTED.
- **Jobs:** keyset scan of ids, read-only; then **one record per tx** with `… FOR NO KEY UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`, re-check the condition under the lock, conditional UPDATE (`WHERE status = …`, must hit 1 row), outbox in the same tx (flag + notification together = no duplicates); time budget ≤ 60 s. Long jobs pick a cron minute no other long job uses (ST20 HIGH needs a free thread).
- **Native SQL:** append only the conditions that were sent (never `:x IS NULL OR …` — it loses the index); keep status literals in the query so partial indexes match; bind `LocalTime` as `CAST(:t AS time)` from `toString()` (Hibernate truncates it to milliseconds otherwise).
- No `lock_timeout` is configured: a deadlock (40P01) → `PessimisticLockingFailureException` → 409.

## Persistence

- Schema follows erd §0:
  - PK `BIGINT GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY` (1–1 tables reuse the FK as PK);
  - FK `<singular>_id`, `ON DELETE RESTRICT`;
  - ROOT / PART / REF tables have `created_at` + `updated_at`, LOG tables only `created_at` and are insert-only;
  - money `BIGINT` VND, business dates `DATE` (Vietnam time), weight `NUMERIC(6,2)`;
  - every enum column `VARCHAR(30)` + `CHECK`.
- Deliberate deviations: erd §13.
- **Flyway owns the schema** (`BE/src/main/resources/db/migration/`, V1 = the whole v16 schema, V2 = 52 [CFG] rows, then seed templates and indexes). Never edit an applied migration.
- **Version numbers are shared with BE-2:** V1–V18 are taken (V13–V15 are BE-2's; V16–V18 are BE-1's and not yet confirmed with BE-2). **Agree on V19 with BE-2 before creating it.**
- A dev DB that ran the old `V13__appointment_code_sequence` needs `docker compose down -v` (ask first — it wipes data) or `flyway repair`.
- The first ADMIN is never seeded: create it with SQL — an `accounts` row (with `phone`) **plus** a `staff_profiles` row (`branch_id` NULL); template in system-overview §1. Don't add bootstrap code for it.

## Testing

- **Unit tests** (`*Test`, JUnit 5 + Mockito) are mandatory for every service or handler that carries a rule or guard. Use `InOrder` for lock → guard → write order, and assert that error branches write nothing.
- **ITs** (`*IT`, `@SpringBootTest`, `test` profile, `@Import(TestcontainersConfiguration.class)`, real Postgres, never H2) are mandatory for every FSM and every complex API.
- FSM tests extend `FsmTransitionTestBase<S>` with an independent copy of the 03 table and **every** valid and invalid pair.
- Get a token in an IT: insert the account with `JdbcTemplate`, then `SessionService.open(...)` inside a `TransactionTemplate`.
- **Isolation between ITs:**
  - Each feature uses its own `MutableClock` era (year 2000 / 2001 / 2003… — see the existing IT) and cleans only that era.
  - Rows get UUID emails or a per-test tag.
  - `audit_logs` cannot be deleted (trigger): tag rows with a random `reason` or narrow the time window.
- **Proven techniques:**

  | Technique | Template |
  |---|---|
  | Race: hold a row lock or advisory lock in another thread, wait on `pg_stat_activity` (`wait_event_type = 'Lock'`, query text `ILIKE '%for no key update%'`), then commit | `whileHeld` / `whileAccountLocked` / `whileBranchesLocked` helpers in `StaffRoleTransferIT`, `BookAppointmentIT`, `StaffProfileIT` |
  | Failure rollback via a temporary trigger that rejects an INSERT / UPDATE | `InAppNotificationIT`, `StaffDisableReactivateIT` |
  | No connection held during BCrypt: `HikariPoolMXBean.getActiveConnections() == 0` inside a `@MockitoSpyBean PasswordEncoder` | `LoginIT`, `PasswordResetIT` |
  | Index proof: `EXPLAIN` on 20 000+ rows inserted in a rolled-back tx + `ANALYZE` | `AuditLogViewIT`, `NoShowIT` |
  | No write happened: compare `xmin` | `AppointmentViewIT`, `BookingRestrictionIT` |
  | Unbuilt module: `@Primary` SQL stand-in implementing the `api` interface | `LinkProfileIT` (`JdbcCustomerModule`), `BookAppointmentIT` |
  | Jobs: era-scoped data, failing batch, `SKIP LOCKED` vs a held row, two concurrent runs | `SessionCleanupIT`, `PendingAccountCleanupIT`, `NoShowIT`, `AppointmentReminderIT` |
  | SMTP: GreenMail on port 3025 | `NotificationOutboxIT` |
  | Contract ↔ Swagger | `OpenApiContractParityIT` (writes `BE/target/openapi.json`) |

- **Mockito traps:**
  - re-stub with `doThrow` / `doAnswer`, never `when(mock.call())`;
  - `@MockitoSpyBean` cannot stub a CGLIB-proxied `@Transactional` bean — spy the repository or encoder instead;
  - to call the real bean from a spy stub, use `mockingDetails(spy).getMockCreationSettings().getDefaultAnswer()`.
- **Mutation testing is expected for concurrency / transaction guarantees:** e.g. remove a lock, add `noRollbackFor`, switch to `REQUIRES_NEW`, use `save` instead of `saveAndFlush`, change the isolation — and a test must turn red. Record the mutations in the ADR.
- **Shared state between ITs:**
  - An IT that changes `system_configs` must restore the V2 value and call `SystemConfigService.reload()`.
  - An IT that edits `notification_templates` must restore `subject = default_subject, body = default_body`.

## Frontend

- **Layout:** `FE/src/` = `app/` (`App.tsx` router + providers) · `pages/<domain>/<Name>Page.tsx` (one file per route; register it in `App.tsx`) · `components/{admin,customer,staff,ui}` · `shared/` (api, stores, hooks, types, utils, constants).
- **State:** React Query for server state, Zustand per domain for app state.
- The FE was built for an older spec. Many routes are out of scope or tier 3 — check `docs/INDEX.md` before wiring a page to an API.
- **HTTP:**
  - One client, `shared/api/axios.ts` (bearer token from `session.store.ts`; 401 ends the session; 400 BR-TK-17 sets `mustChangePassword`). Errors go through `shared/api/api-error.ts` (`ApiError` with the 6 envelope fields + `ruleId`).
  - `auth.api.ts`, `profile.api.ts`, `catalog.api.ts`, `branch.api.ts` follow the contracts. The **contract mock** `shared/api/mock/` is on in `npm run dev` (demo password `Petcare123`, every OTP `123456`); set `VITE_API_MOCK=false` to call the real BE.
  - Staff workspaces still use `shared/api/clinic.api.ts` → `clinic-db.ts`, an in-browser `localStorage` mock. Replace it function by function once each BE endpoint exists.
- **Routing:**
  - `RequireRole` handles no session → login, `mustChangePassword` → change password, wrong role → 403.
  - `RequireLinkDecision` handles BR-TK-19.
  - Staff area = `StaffShell` for `/staff/*` workspaces and `/admin/*`; menu roles come from `shared/constants/staff-menu.ts`.

## Gotchas

<<<<<<< HEAD
- **An IT that edits `notification_templates` must restore `subject = default_subject, body = default_body`** in `@AfterEach` (`SchemaMigrationIT.seededNotificationTemplatesAreRestorableAndConsistent` goes red otherwise); a new seed template must pass the strict variable grammar of ADR-0037 (`NotificationTemplateAdminIT.everySeededDefaultPassesValidation`).
- **An IT that changes `system_configs` must restore the V2 value and call `SystemConfigService.reload()`** (context and DB are shared; `SystemConfigIT.seedMatchesCatalogExactly` goes red otherwise). Via the API, the cross-key pairs of `ConfigConstraints` apply — setting a value by SQL that breaks a pair makes the next `reload()` (and app start) fail.
- **Lock order of the sales module** (ADR-0044): caller's own document (Visit) → `orders` (lock the row **before** reading it) → `products` `FOR SHARE` → stock lots (ADR-0042). REST in `sales` never locks `visits`; a payment (T30) must lock several orders by ascending id. Order totals are recomputed from the lines, never incremented.
- **Lock order of the inventory module** (ADR-0042): caller's own document (Order, Visit) → `stock_receipts` → `suppliers` `FOR SHARE` → `products` `FOR SHARE` → `inventory_items` / `stock_lots` creation (`ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`) → all lots in **one** `ORDER BY id FOR NO KEY UPDATE` statement → `StockLedger.apply`. Never `UPDATE stock_lots` outside `StockLedger`; never lock lots one product at a time.
- **Lock order of the customer module `accounts → customers (id ↑) → pets → weight_records`** (ADR-0041): take the pet row lock (`FOR NO KEY UPDATE`) before asking visit / boarding / sales, and in other modules call `PetQueryApi.findPetForShare` before inserting a row that points at a pet — FK `FOR KEY SHARE` does not conflict with `FOR NO KEY UPDATE`. Never `findById` a `Pet` / `Customer` before locking it (stale persistence context).
- **Lock order of staff administration `branches (id ↑) → accounts → staff_profiles → sessions`** (ADR-0029): lock branches only through `BranchApi.lockForStaffChange`, before `accounts`; never `catch` its lock failure inside the tx (rollback-only). Every flow that can lower a branch's ACTIVE BRANCH_MANAGER count locks the branch first, even when DRAFT.
- **Lock order `accounts → sessions`** (ADR-0021): every flow that revokes sessions (logout, change / reset password, lock, disable) locks or writes the `accounts` row **before** `UPDATE sessions`; the reverse order deadlocks with them (`LogoutIT.logoutDoesNotDeadlockWithAccountsFirstRevoke` goes red). Use `@Modifying` queries in that order — an entity setter on `Account` is flushed later and silently reverses it.
- **Open Session In View is off** (ADR-0020): map entities to DTOs inside the service transaction; a lazy access in a controller now throws `LazyInitializationException`. With OSIV on, Spring's `DELAYED_ACQUISITION_AND_HOLD` keeps a connection for the whole request (`LoginIT.bcryptRunsWithoutHoldingConnection` goes red).
- **BCrypt `matches` compares only the first 72 bytes** (spring-security-crypto 6.5.11 `checkpw` skips the length check that `encode` does): treat a longer input as a wrong password before matching (`LoginAttemptService.fitsBcrypt`, ADR-0019 §7).
- **`InvalidCredentialsException` (401) and `CurrentPasswordMismatchException` (400 BR-TK-14)** must be thrown directly inside their `noRollbackFor` method, after its writes — same trap as `OtpRejectedException` below.
- **`findByIdForUpdate` / `findByEmailForUpdate` emit `FOR NO KEY UPDATE`** (Hibernate 6, `PESSIMISTIC_WRITE` on PostgreSQL), not `FOR UPDATE`: it still conflicts with every other row lock except FK `FOR KEY SHARE`; ITs that wait on `pg_stat_activity` must match that text.
- **`OtpRejectedException` through a `@Transactional` without `noRollbackFor`** marks the transaction rollback-only: the caller's commit then throws `UnexpectedRollbackException` → 500 instead of 400, and the `failed_attempts` increment is lost (ADR-0010; proven by mutation on `RegistrationVerificationIT`).
- **Port mismatch.** `mvn spring-boot:run` listens on 8081 (`SERVER_PORT` default) and axios defaults to 8081, but the Vite `/api` proxy defaults to 8080 (Docker; nginx in the FE image proxies `/api/` → `backend:8080/api/`). Set `VITE_API_URL` consistently when running BE locally.
- **CI is broken for BE** (`.github/workflows/ci.yml`): the `mvn flyway:migrate` step has no Flyway Maven plugin in `pom.xml`; its Postgres service and `-Dflyway.*` flags are now unnecessary (tests use Testcontainers). It triggers on `main, develop`, but the working branch is `dev`. FE lint runs with `|| true`.

- **Large files:** `docs/architecture/system-overview.md` (~190 KB) and `docs/02-business-rules.md` (~80 KB) — read them by section (Grep the heading or rule id), never whole.
- **Port mismatch:** `mvn spring-boot:run` and axios default to 8081; the Vite proxy and Docker use 8080. Set `VITE_API_URL` consistently.
- **CI is broken for BE:** `.github/workflows/ci.yml` calls a Flyway Maven plugin that doesn't exist and triggers on `main, develop` (the working branch is `dev`); FE lint runs with `|| true`. Verify locally (`mvn clean verify` + docker smoke).
- **OSIV is off** (ADR-0020): map to DTOs inside the service tx; a lazy access in a controller throws.
- **BCrypt `matches` only compares the first 72 bytes:** reject longer input before matching.
- **`docs/dept/` is gitignored:** BE-2 never sees the debt files. Anything BE-2 needs goes into `docs/06-module-contracts.md` §6. Don't un-ignore it without asking.
- **Skills** live in `.opencode/skills/` (`designing-apis`, `postman-api-testing`); there is no `.claude/skills/`.
- `hs_err_pid*.log` / `replay_pid*.log` are JVM crash dumps; `.ua/` is tooling scratch.

## After finishing a change

1. Contract change: edit `docs/api/generator/m_<module>.py`, regenerate, run the check. Cross-module interface change: `docs/06-module-contracts.md`.
2. New technical decision: `docs/adr/000n-*.md` + `docs/adr/INDEX.md` (+ the one-line list in `docs/INDEX.md` §5).
3. Debt left or paid: `docs/dept/D<nnn>-*.md` + `docs/dept/INDEX.md`.
4. State: `docs/architecture/system-overview.md` (detail), `docs/time.md` (task status).
5. **This file:** only when something system-wide changes — the state table, a lock order, a platform tool, a new gotcha, the next free V / ADR number. Keep feature history out of it.
6. Never `git add` / `commit` / `push` unless the user asks.
